text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"@'\nimport json,pathlib,hashlib,re,collections\nroot=pathlib.Path('audits/skills-2026-10-09')\np=root/'global-review.json'\nd=json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\nreceipts=[]\nfor f in root.glob('claude*review.json'):\n    obj=json.loads(f.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n    receipts.extend(obj if isinstance(obj,list) else obj.get('rows',[]))\nreviewed={}\nfor x in receipts:\n    fp=pathlib.Path(x.get('path',''))\n    expected=str(x.get('sha256',x.get('hash',''))).lower()\n    if fp.is_file() and 'full body' in x.get('semantic_review','').lower() and hashlib.sha256(fp.read_bytes()).hexdigest()==expected:\n        reviewed[str(fp.resolve()).lower()]=x\nbases={}\nfor r in ['claude','codex','gemini']:\n    for f in pathlib.Path('skills',r).glob('*/SKILL.md'):\n        text=f.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')\n        h=hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()\n        bases.setdefault(h,[]).append(f)\nfull_names={'firecrawl-search','firecrawl-seo-audit','firecrawl-shop','browser','docs','docx','frontend-design','grill-me','import-memory','web-artifacts-builder','imagegen','openai-docs','review-agent','skill-creator','skill-audit','lsat-u-platform-actions'}\nreasons={\n'firecrawl-search':('fix','Search workflow is useful; repair Bash-only command example for Windows and make feedback transmission opt-in or explicitly covered by invoked workflow. Paid hosted service, not free local search.'),\n'firecrawl-seo-audit':('keep','Focused SEO evidence collection; distinct from generic crawl. Hosted API dependencies and ranking conclusions remain untested.'),\n'firecrawl-shop':('keep','Distinct personal shopping workflow with cart authorization and checkout boundary; hosted paid API availability untested.'),\n'browser':('fix','Useful CDP transport and documented profile limits. Default public contribution PR and paid remote daemon need task-scoped authorization; preserve local browser tools and secrets boundary.'),\n'docs':('fix','Overbroad document catchall and mandatory creation before any source inspection; provider substitutions introduce Codex.ai artifact claim and unavailable docs-connector assumptions.'),\n'docx':('keep','Useful deterministic Word mechanics; resolve preinstalled runtime through current dependency tool, Bash examples need Windows adaptation; avoid unconditional native-doc takeover.'),\n'frontend-design':('narrow-trigger','Old synced preset bans normal fonts and attracts all frontend work; prefer current maintained counterpart for ordinary product work while preserving explicit creative preset use.'),\n'grill-me':('keep','Checkpointed interview is useful and distinct from review/execution; older synced variant should route to current project capture workflow; Bash date is only fallback.'),\n'import-memory':('repair','Provider substitution turns Anthropic memory workflow into Codex memory tools/Settings URL; actual Codex native notes workflow differs. Preserve additive conflict handling and pasted-data boundary.'),\n'web-artifacts-builder':('fix','Useful bundler; altered Codex.ai artifact claim, relative app creation risks vault boundary, and discouraging upfront testing conflict relevant verification. Keep outputs in sibling app root.'),\n'imagegen':('keep','Current system imagegen supports built-in real transparency, explicit API fallback, and non-destructive asset handling; this current body supersedes earlier stale catalog chromakey guidance.'),\n'openai-docs':('keep','Focused current official evidence guidance and generic-task exclusion; docs-first order conflicts local-first project preference and should be honored by runtime higher-priority instructions, not silently transferred wholesale.'),\n'review-agent':('keep','Read-only diff review preserves scope and discrete demonstrated regressions; distinct from library-wide skill inspection.'),\n'skill-creator':('keep','Current system creator has precise discovery, scope preservation and optional forward testing; synced Anthropic creator is a different eval/package workflow. Preserve both only with clear target environment.'),\n'skill-audit':('keep','Evidence-oriented audit/evaluate/apply distinction, no usage inference from mentions, scoped backups and audit-only no-mutation; strong reusable complement to current inspection.'),\n'lsat-u-platform-actions':('repair','Global browser-only legacy workflow contradicts maintained headless Node/MCP project route, carries old teacher?ws URLs and cross-runtime tool assumptions; retain UI fallback mechanics in current canonical route.'),\n'claude-api':('repair','Exact mechanical Claude→Codex substitution corrupts provider identity, Anthropic package/model identifiers and paths; broad agent/NL triggers can select it outside API work. Restore Anthropic identity and narrow scope.'),\n'booking-link':('repair','Global variant restores projects/lsatu path inside vault instead of sibling app root; route to maintained project copy.'),\n'find-skills':('repair','Useful skill search; mechanical provider replacement corrupts literal ComposioHQ/awesome-claude-skills repo reference.'),\n'algorithmic-art':('repair','Useful generative art workflow; mechanical provider replacement introduces Codex.ai artifact destination claims. Preserve algorithm mechanism while adapt host presentation.'),\n'canvas-design':('keep','Provider prose-only substitutions do not alone change canvas capability; keep specialized art workflow, inherited canonical limitations remain.'),\n'doc-coauthoring':('keep','Distinct iterative coauthoring workflow; provider substitutions mostly names, inherit reviewed canonical scope/trigger findings.'),\n'executing-plans':('keep','Plan execution remains distinct from planning; inherited base scope and explicit delegation authorization still apply.'),\n'graphify':('repair','Useful graph capability; automatic AGENTS.md integration and altered Codex desktop naming need scope/manual safeguards. Global variant not proof graph runtime works.'),\n'lsat-lab-mega-set':('repair','Distinct consolidated PrepTest set workflow; Codex-in-chrome invented by provider replacement, use actual available browser transport and authenticated verification.'),\n'prompt-architect-refiner':('repair','Useful prompt refinement but mechanical Codex Cowork naming and duplicated Codex modes distort target product distinctions; scope to user chosen runtime.'),\n'using-superpowers':('repair','Global always-load/invoke Skill assumption and provider substitutions require compatible host adapter; do not treat every request as permission to invoke unavailable tool.'),\n'chat-export':('repair','Global output paths differ from current handoffs home and claim every verbatim message from current context; must retrieve available logs or disclose missing compacted history.'),\n'lsat-u-architecture':('repair','Legacy app root inside vault and stale authority bundle compete with current app-root instruction routing; backend correctness needs authoritative comparison, not inferred from old date.'),\n'lsat-u-topic-builder':('repair','Useful audited topic bootstrap; global paths restore Mac roots or vault-contained app output, provider products/templates require current runtime adaptation.'),\n'lsatu-cc-handoff':('repair','Useful code-backed build brief; unconditional commit/push wording and raw-source brief writes/archive moves violate task-scoped authority and source preservation. Use current handoff/workproduct home.'),\n'pull-submissions':('repair','Global script route uses legacy catalog/runtime and projects/lsatu env path inside vault; reconnect to actual sibling app dependency without exposing keys.'),\n}\nchanged=0\nfor x in d['rows']:\n    semantic=x.get('semantic_review','')\n    if not (semantic.startswith('partial') or semantic.startswith('inherited-identical')): continue\n    f=pathlib.Path(x['path']); text=f.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')\n    x['sha256']=hashlib.sha256(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest(); x['normalized_text_sha256']=hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()\n    name=x['name'].strip(chr(34)); folder=f.parent.name\n    if folder=='hatch-pet': continue\n    exact=bases.get(x['normalized_text_sha256'],[])\n    base=next((b for b in exact if str(b.resolve()).lower() in reviewed),None)\n    transformed=False\n    if base is None and not exact:\n        candidate=pathlib.Path('skills/claude',folder,'SKILL.md')\n        if candidate.exists(): base=candidate; transformed=True\n    r=reviewed.get(str(base.resolve()).lower()) if base else None\n    direct=(name in full_names and ('synced' in str(f) or '.system' in str(f) or name in {'firecrawl-search','firecrawl-seo-audit','firecrawl-shop','skill-audit','lsat-u-platform-actions'}))\n    if direct:\n        x['semantic_review']='full-body-read'; x['semantic_evidence_path']=str(f); x['coverage']='Entire SKILL.md read in bounded sections; no target runtime execution'\n    elif base:\n        x['semantic_base_path']=str(base.resolve()); x['semantic_base_sha256']=hashlib.sha256(base.read_bytes()).hexdigest()\n        if transformed:\n            x['semantic_review']='full-variant-difference-reviewed; inherits completed base' if r else 'full-variant-difference-reviewed; awaiting completed base'\n            x['coverage']='Complete provider substitution comparison and all additional difference hunks inspected; base completion required'\n            x['variant_transform']='case-insensitive Claude Code→Codex then Claude→Codex, plus complete residual diff reviewed'\n        else:\n            x['semantic_review']='inherited-identical-full-text-completed-base' if r else 'awaiting completed normalized-identical canonical base'\n            x['coverage']='Entire normalized text identical; only line-ending bytes differ; base completion required'\n        x['semantic_evidence_path']=str(base.resolve())\n    else:\n        continue\n    disposition,reason=reasons.get(folder,reasons.get(name,('keep','Distinct capability; inherit completed matching canonical semantic disposition and support/runtime limitations.')))\n    if r and not transformed and not direct:\n        disposition=r.get('disposition',disposition); reason=r.get('reason',reason)\n    x['disposition']=disposition if direct or r else 'pending-base-review'\n    x['reason']=reason\n    x['confidence']='High for inspected instruction/difference findings; live workflow unverified' if direct or r else 'variant reviewed; canonical base completion pending'\n    x['support_coverage']='SKILL.md body or full reviewed-base plus complete difference; local reference existence scan. Supporting payloads not exhaustively inspected or executed.'\n    changed+=1\n# Propagate own verified full physical or transformed body receipts to exact global duplicate rows, only completed semantic bases.\nbyhash={x['sha256']:x for x in d['rows'] if x.get('semantic_review') in {'full-body-read','inherited-identical-full-text-completed-base','full-variant-difference-reviewed; inherits completed base'}}\nfor x in d['rows']:\n    src=byhash.get(x['sha256'])\n    if src and src is not x and x.get('semantic_review','').startswith('partial'):\n        for key in ['disposition','reason','confidence','semantic_evidence_path','semantic_base_path','semantic_base_sha256','variant_transform']:\n            if key in src:x[key]=src[key]\n        x['semantic_review']='inherited-exact-byte-hash-completed-review'; x['coverage']='Exact physical SHA256 match to completed body review'\nd['semantic_counts']=dict(collections.Counter(x.get('semantic_review') for x in d['rows']))\nd['status']='Global continuation reviewed remaining unique bodies/differences; final canonical inheritance and hatch-pet review awaiting integration'\np.write_text(json.dumps(d,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\\n',encoding='utf-8')\nprint('updated',changed,'semantic_counts',d['semantic_counts'])\nprint('still partial',[(x['name'],x['path']) for x in d['rows'] if x.get('semantic_review','').startswith('partial')])\n'@ | python -X utf8 -",max_output_tokens:2200}));

## Tool: custom_tool_call_output

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.3 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"6a9c01","wall_time_seconds":0.352402,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":857,"output":"---\\r\\nname: skill-installer\\r\\ndescription: Install Codex skills into $CODEX_HOME/skills from a curated list or a GitHub repo path. Use when a user asks to list installable skills, install a curated skill, or install a skill from another repo (including private repos).\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  short-description: Install curated skills from openai/skills or other repos\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Skill Installer\\r\\n\\r\\nHelps install skills. By default these are from https://github.com/openai/skills/tree/main/skills/.curated, but users can also provide other locations. Experimental skills live in https://github.com/openai/skills/tree/main/skills/.experimental and can be installed the same way.\\r\\n\\r\\nUse the helper scripts based on the task:\\r\\n- List skills when the user asks what is available, or if the user uses this skill without specifying what to do. Default listing is `.curated`, but you can pass `--path skills/.experimental` when they ask about experimental skills.\\r\\n- Install from the curated list when the user provides a skill name.\\r\\n- Install from another repo when the user provides a GitHub repo/path (including private repos).\\r\\n\\r\\nInstall skills with the helper scripts.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Communication\\r\\n\\r\\nWhen listing skills, output approximately as follows, depending on the context of the user\'s request. If they ask about experimental skills, list from `.experimental` instead of `.curated` and label the source accordingly:\\r\\n\\"\\"\\"\\r\\nSkills from {repo}:\\r\\n1. skill-1\\r\\n2. skill-2 (already installed)\\r\\n3. ...\\r\\nWhich ones would you like installed?\\r\\n\\"\\"\\"\\r\\n\\r\\nAfter installing a skill, tell the user it will be available on their next turn.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Scripts\\r\\n\\r\\nAll of these scripts use network, so when running in the sandbox, request escalation when running them.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `scripts/list-skills.py` (prints skills list with installed annotations)\\r\\n- `scripts/list-skills.py --format json`\\r\\n- Example (experimental list): `scripts/list-skills.py --path skills/.experimental`\\r\\n- `scripts/install-skill-from-github.py --repo <owner>/<repo> --path <path/to/skill> [<path/to/skill> ...]`\\r\\n- `scripts/install-skill-from-github.py --url https://github.com/<owner>/<repo>/tree/<ref>/<path>`\\r\\n- Example (experimental skill): `scripts/install-skill-from-github.py --repo openai/skills --path skills/.experimental/<skill-name>`\\r\\n\\r\\n## Behavior and Options\\r\\n\\r\\n- Defaults to direct download for public GitHub repos.\\r\\n- If download fails with auth/permission errors, falls back to git sparse checkout.\\r\\n- Aborts if the destination skill directory already exists.\\r\\n- Installs into `$CODEX_HOME/skills/<skill-name>` (defaults to `~/.codex/skills`).\\r\\n- Multiple `--path` values install multiple skills in one run, each named from the path basename unless `--name` is supplied.\\r\\n- Options: `--ref <ref>` (default `main`), `--dest <path>`, `--method auto|download|git`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Notes\\r\\n\\r\\n- Curated listing is fetched from `https://github.com/openai/skills/tree/main/skills/.curated` via the GitHub API. If it is unavailable, explain the error and exit.\\r\\n- Private GitHub repos can be accessed via existing git credentials or optional `GITHUB_TOKEN`/`GH_TOKEN` for download.\\r\\n- Git fallback tries HTTPS first, then SSH.\\r\\n- The skills at https://github.com/openai/skills/tree/main/skills/.system are preinstalled, so no need to help users install those. If they ask, just explain this. If they insist, you can download and overwrite.\\r\\n- Installed annotations come from `$CODEX_HOME/skills`.\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

This corrupts Anthropic API identity/model/package/path strings; the same transform also changes literal repository links, Codex.ai artifact destinations, Codex Cowork product labels and nonexistent browser-tool naming in several skills. Its provider-substituted Codex.ai links and mandatory ordering need host compatibility and explicit document-product intent.\n- **Synced import-memory is a transformed Anthropic workflow**, requiring memory_write/memory_append and a Codex.ai settings importer. Preserve additive conflict/data handling but do not advertise these as current native Codex tools or replace the actual authorized native-memory note route.\n- **Synced frontend-design is older and stronger than maintained design guidance**, banning ordinary fonts and requiring unusual creative styling for generic UI work. Preserve this current system capability; no API model availability claims were independently tested.\n- **Global chat-export promises full verbatim conversation reconstruction from current context** and uses old Mac/Documents destinations. Use available logs or disclose compacted/missing sections; keep current handoffs home.\n- **Browser harness domain-skills opt-in includes default public PR contribution** and remote paid sessions. These require actual task-scoped authorization; local CDP capability remains useful.\n- **System skill-audit is worth preserving**: it separates inspection from measured performance and changes, refuses usage inference from mentions, and labels discovery versus semantic coverage. Version is optional — agent name alone is fine. Do not omit this header or send a bare default — Cloudflare will return a 403 (error code 1010) and block the request.\\r\\n\\r\\n## API Reference\\r\\n\\r\\nFull OpenAPI spec: [transcriptapi.com/openapi.json](https://transcriptapi.com/openapi.json) — consult this for the latest parameters and schemas.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Transcript — 1 credit\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/transcript\\\\\\r\\n?video_url=VIDEO_URL&format=text&include_timestamp=true&send_metadata=true\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Search — 1 credit\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/search?q=QUERY&type=video&limit=10\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n| Param   | Default | Values                 |\\r\\n| ------- | ------- | ---------------------- |\\r\\n| `q`     | —       | 1-200 chars (required) |\\r\\n| `type`  | `video` | `video`, `channel`     |\\r\\n| `limit` | `20`    | 1-50                   |\\r\\n\\r\\n## Channel latest — FREE\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/latest?channel=@TED\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nReturns last 15 videos with exact view counts and publish dates. Accepts `@handle`, channel URL, or `UC...` ID.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Resolve handle — FREE\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/resolve?input=@TED\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nUse to convert @handle to UC... channel ID.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Errors\\r\\n\\r\\n| Code     | Meaning          | Action                                         |\\r\\n| -------- | ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |\\r\\n| 401      | Bad API key      | Check key                                      |\\r\\n| 402      | No credits       | transcriptapi.com/billing                      |\\r\\n| 403/1010 | Cloudflare block | Add or fix User-Agent header                   |\\r\\n| 404      | Not found        | No captions or resource doesn\'t exist          |\\r\\n| 408      | Timeout          | Retry once                                     |\\r\\n\\r\\nFree tier: 100 credits. Search and transcript cost 1 credit. Channel latest and resolve are free.\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

   |\\n| `include_timestamp` | no       | `true`  | `true` or `false`               |\\n| `send_metadata`     | no       | `false` | `true` or `false`               |\\n\\nAccepts: `https://youtube.com/watch?v=ID`, `https://youtu.be/ID`, `youtube.com/shorts/ID`, or bare `ID`.\\n\\n**Response** (`format=json`):\\n\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\"video_id\\": \\"dQw4w9WgXcQ\\",\\n \\"author_name\\": \\"...\\", \\"author_url\\": \\"...\\" }\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### GET /api/v2/youtube/search — 1 credit\\n\\n```bash\\ncurl -s YourAgent/1.0\\"\\n```\\n\\n| Param   | Required | Default | Validation            |\\n| ------- | -------- | ------- | --------------------- |\\n| `q`     | yes      | —       | 1-200 chars (trimmed) |\\n| `type`  | no       | `video` | `video` or `channel`  |\\n| `limit` | no       | `20`    | 1-50                  |\\n\\n**Response** (`type=video`):\\n\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\"results\\": [\\n    {\\n      \\"type\\": \\"video\\",\\n      \\"videoId\\": \\"dQw4w9WgXcQ\\",\\n      \\"title\\": \\"Rick Astley - Never Gonna Give You Up\\",\\n      \\"channelId\\": \\"UCuAXFkgsw1L7xaCfnd5JJOw\\",\\n      \\"channelTitle\\": \\"Rick Astley\\",\\n      \\"channelHandle\\": \\"@RickAstley\\",\\n      \\"channelVerified\\": true,\\n      \\"lengthText\\": \\"width\\": 120, \\"height\\": 90 }]\\n    }\\n  ],\\n  \\"result_count\\": 20\\n}\\n```\\n\\n**Response** (`type=channel`):\\n\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\"results\\": [\\n    {\\n      \\"type\\": \\"channel\\",\\n      \\"channelId\\": \\"UCuAXFkgsw1L7xaCfnd5JJOw\\",\\n      \\"title\\": \\"Rick Astley\\",\\n      \\"handle\\": \\"@RickAstley\\",\\n      \\"subscriberCount\\": \\"4.2M subscribers\\",\\n      \\"verified\\": true,\\n      \\"rssUrl\\": \\"https://www.youtube.com/feeds/videos.xml?channel_id=UC...\\"\\n    }\\n  ],\\n  \\"result_count\\": 5\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### GET /api/v2/youtube/channel/resolve — FREE (0 credits)\\n\\n```bash\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/resolve?input=@TED\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\n```\\n\\n| Param   | Required | Validation                              |\\n| ------- | -------- | --------------------------------------- |\\n| `input` | yes      | 1-200 chars — @handle, URL, or UC... ID |\\n\\n**Response:**\\n\\n```json\\n{ \\"channel_id\\": \\"UCsT0YIqwnpJCM-mx7-gSA4Q\\", \\"resolved_from\\": \\"@TED\\" }\\n```\\n\\nIf input is already a valid `UC[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{22}` ID, returns immediately without lookup.\\n\\n### GET /api/v2/youtube/channel/videos — 1 credit/page\\n\\n```bash\\n# First page (100 videos)\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/videos?channel=@NASA\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\n\\n# Next pages\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/videos?continuation=TOKEN\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\n```\\n\\n| Param          | Required    | Validation                                    |\\n| -------------- | ----------- | --------------------------------------------- |\\n| `channel`      | conditional | `@handle`, channel URL, or `UC...` ID         |\\n| `continuation` | conditional | non-empty string (next pages)                 |\\n\\nProvide exactly one of `channel` or `continuation`.\\n\\n**Response:**\\n\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\"results\\": [{\\n    \\"videoId\\": \\"abc123xyz00\\",\\n    \\"title\\": \\"Latest Video\\",\\n    \\"channelId\\": \\"UCsT0YIqwnpJCM-mx7-gSA4Q\\",\\n    \\"channelTitle\\": \\"TED\\",\\n    \\"channelHandle\\": \\"@TED\\",\\n    \\"lengthText\\": \\"15:22\\",\\n    \\"viewCountText\\": \\"3.2M views\\",\\n    \\"thumbnails\\": [...],\\n    \\"index\\": \\"0\\"\\n  }],\\n  \\"playlist_info\\": {\\"title\\": \\"Uploads from TED\\", \\"numVideos\\": \\"5000\\"},\\n  \\"continuation_token\\": \\"4qmFsgKlARIYVVV1...\\",\\n  \\"has_more\\": true\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### GET /api/v2/youtube/channel/latest — FREE (0 credits)\\n\\n```bash\\ncurl -s Param     | Required | Validation                                |\\n| --------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------- |\\n| `channel` | yes      | `@handle`, channel URL, or `UC...` ID     |\\n\\nReturns last 15 videos via RSS with exact view counts and ISO timestamps.\\n\\n**Response:**\\n\\n```json\\n{\\n  \\"channel\\": {\\n    \\"channelId\\": \\"...\\",\\n    \\"title\\": \\"TED\\",\\n    \\"author\\": \\"TED\\",\\n    \\"url\\": \\"...\\"\\n  },\\n  \\"results\\": [\\n    {\\n      \\"videoId\\": \\"abc123xyz00\\",\\n      \\"title\\": \\"Latest Video\\",\\n      \\"published\\": \\"2026-01-30T16:00:00Z\\",\\n      \\"viewCount\\": \\"2287630\\",\\n      \\"description\\": \\"Full description...\\",\\n      \\"thumbnail\\": { \\"url\\": \\"...\\", \\"width\\": \\"480\\", \\"height\\": \\"360\\" }\\n    }\\n  ],\\n  \\"result_count\\": 15\\n}\\n```\\n\\n### GET /api/v2/youtube/channel/search — 1 credit\\n\\n```bash\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/search\\\\\\n?channel=@TED&q=climate+change&limit=30\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\n```\\n\\n| Param     | Required | Validation                                |\\n| --------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------- |\\n| `channel` | yes      | `@handle`, channel URL, or `UC...` ID     |\\n| `q`       | yes      | 1-200 chars                               |\\n| `limit`   | no       | 1-50 (default 30)                         |\\n\\n### GET /api/v2/youtube/playlist/videos — 1 credit/page\\n\\n```bash\\n# First page\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/playlist/videos?playlist=PL_PLAYLIST_ID\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\n\\n# Next pages\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/playlist/videos?continuation=TOKEN\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\n```\\n\\n| Param          | Required    | Validation                                           |\\n| -------------- | ----------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |\\n| `playlist`     | conditional | Playlist URL or ID (`PL`/`UU`/`LL`/`FL`/`OL` prefix) |\\n| `continuation` | conditional | non-empty string                                     |\\n\\n## Credit Costs\\n\\n| Endpoint        | Cost     |\\n| --------------- | -------- |\\n| transcript      | 1        |\\n| search          | 1        |\\n| channel/resolve | **free** |\\n| channel/search  | 1        |\\n| channel/videos  | 1/page   |\\n| channel/latest  | **free** |\\n| playlist/videos | 1/page   |\\n\\n## Errors\\n\\n| Code     | Meaning           | Action                                              |\\n| -------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |\\n| 401      | Bad API key       | Check key, re-run setup                             |\\n| 402      | No credits        | Top up at transcriptapi.com/billing                 |\\n| 403/1010 | Cloudflare block  | Add or fix User-Agent header                        |\\n| 404      | Not found         | Video/channel/playlist doesn\'t exist or no captions |\\n| 408      | Timeout/retryable | Retry once after 2s                                 |\\n| 422      | Validation error  | Check param format                                  |\\n| 429      | Rate limited      | Wait, respect Retry-After                           |\\n\\n## Tips\\n\\n- When user shares YouTube URL with no instruction, fetch transcript and summarize key points.\\n- Use `channel/latest` (free) to check for new uploads before fetching transcripts — pass @handle directly.\\n- For research: search → pick videos → fetch transcripts.\\n- Free tier: 100 credits, 300 req/min. Starter ($5/mo): 1,000 credits, 300 req/min.\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

No additional runtimes or dependencies needed.\\r\\nrequired_environment_variables:\\r\\n  - name: TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\r\\n    prompt: Your TranscriptAPI key (starts with sk_)\\r\\n    help: Free account at https://transcriptapi.com — 100 credits, no card required. Or let the agent create one for you.\\r\\n    required_for: all API requests\\r\\nmetadata: {\\"openclaw\\":{\\"emoji\\":\\"▶️\\",\\"requires\\":{\\"env\\":[\\"TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\"]},\\"primaryEnv\\":\\"TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\",\\"homepage\\":\\"https://transcriptapi.com\\"},\\"hermes\\":{\\"tags\\":[\\"youtube\\",\\"transcripts\\",\\"video\\",\\"search\\",\\"channels\\",\\"playlists\\",\\"api\\",\\"no-quota\\"],\\"category\\":\\"media\\"}}\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# YouTube API\\r\\n\\r\\nYouTube data access via [TranscriptAPI.com](https://transcriptapi.com) — no Google API quota needed.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Setup\\r\\n\\r\\nIf `$TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY` is not set, read [references/auth-setup.md](references/auth-setup.md) and follow the instructions there to get and store the key.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Required Headers\\r\\n\\r\\nEvery request needs two headers:\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Authorization:[REDACTED] `Bearer $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY`\\r\\n- **User-Agent:** your agent\'s name and version if known (e.g. `HermesAgent/0.11.0`, `ClaudeCode/1.0`). Version is optional — agent name alone is fine. Do not omit this header or send a bare default — Cloudflare will return a 403 (error code 1010) and block the request.\\r\\n\\r\\n## API Reference\\r\\n\\r\\nFull OpenAPI spec: [transcriptapi.com/openapi.json](https://transcriptapi.com/openapi.json) — consult this for the latest parameters and schemas.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Endpoint Reference\\r\\n\\r\\nAll endpoints: `https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/...`\\r\\n\\r\\nChannel endpoints accept `channel` — an `@handle`, channel URL, or `UC...` ID. Playlist endpoints accept `playlist` — a playlist URL or ID.\\r\\n\\r\\n| Endpoint                            | Method | Cost     |\\r\\n| ----------------------------------- | ------ | -------- |\\r\\n| `/transcript?video_url=ID`          | GET    | 1        |\\r\\n| `/search?q=QUERY&type=video`        | GET    | 1        |\\r\\n| `/channel/resolve?input=@handle`    | GET    | **free** |\\r\\n| `/channel/latest?channel=@handle`   | GET    | **free** |\\r\\n| `/channel/videos?channel=@handle`   | GET    | 1/page   |\\r\\n| `/channel/search?channel=@handle&q=Q` | GET  | 1        |\\r\\n| `/playlist/videos?playlist=PL_ID`   | GET    | 1/page   |\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quick Examples\\r\\n\\r\\n**Search videos:**\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/search\\\\\\r\\n?q=python+tutorial&type=video&limit=10\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n**Get transcript:**\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/transcript\\\\\\r\\n?video_url=dQw4w9WgXcQ&format=text&include_timestamp=true&send_metadata=true\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n**Resolve channel handle (free):**\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/resolve?input=@TED\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n**Latest videos (free):**\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/latest?channel=@TED\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n**Browse channel uploads (paginated):**\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/videos?channel=@NASA\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n# Use continuation token from response for next pages\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n**Browse playlist (paginated):**\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/playlist/videos?playlist=PL_PLAYLIST_ID\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parameter Validation\\r\\n\\r\\n| Field          | Rule                                                    |\\r\\n| -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |\\r\\n| `channel`      | `@handle`, channel URL, or `UC...` ID                   |\\r\\n| `playlist`     | Playlist URL or ID |\\r\\n| `continuation` | non-empty string                                        |\\r\\n\\r\\n## Why Not Google\'s API?\\r\\n\\r\\n|             | Google YouTube Data API         | TranscriptAPI OAuth + API key + project       | Single API key             |\\r\\n| Transcripts | Not available                   | Core feature               |\\r\\n| Pricing     | $0.0015/unit overage            | $5/1000 credits            |\\r\\n\\r\\n## Errors\\r\\n\\r\\n| Code     | Meaning           | Action                                         |\\r\\n| -------- | ----------------- | limited      | Wait, respect Retry-After                      |\\r\\n\\r\\nFree tier: 100 credits, 300 req/min. Starter ($5/mo): 1,000 credits.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE youtube-data \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: youtube-data\\r\\ndescription: \\"Use when structured YouTube data is needed: pasted video/channel/playlist links, transcripts for analysis, video metadata, channel upload history, search results, or playlist contents — without Google API quotas or OAuth. No additional runtimes or dependencies needed.\\r\\nrequired_environment_variables:\\r\\n  - name: TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\r\\n    prompt: Your TranscriptAPI key (starts with sk_)\\r\\n    help: Free account at https://transcriptapi.com — 100 credits, no card required. Or let the agent create one for you.\\r\\n    required_for: all API requests\\r\\nmetadata: {\\"openclaw\\":{\\"emoji\\":\\"▶️\\",\\"requires\\":{\\"env\\":[\\"TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\"]},\\"primaryEnv\\":\\"TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\",\\"homepage\\":\\"https://transcriptapi.com\\"},\\"hermes\\":{\\"tags\\":[\\"youtube\\",\\"transcripts\\",\\"video\\",\\"search\\",\\"channels\\",\\"playlists\\",\\"data\\",\\"metadata\\"],\\"category\\":\\"media\\"}}\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# YouTube Data\\r\\n\\r\\nYouTube data access via [TranscriptAPI.com](https://transcriptapi.com) — lightweight alternative to Google\'s YouTube Data API.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Setup\\r\\n\\r\\nIf `$TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY` is not set, read [references/auth-setup.md](references/auth-setup.md) and follow the instructions there to get and store the key.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Required Headers\\r\\n\\r\\nEvery request needs two headers:\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Authorization:[REDACTED] `Bearer $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY`\\r\\n- **User-Agent:** your agent\'s name and version if known (e.g. `HermesAgent/0.11.0`, `ClaudeCode/1.0`). Version is optional — agent name alone is fine. Do not omit this header or send a bare default — Cloudflare will return a 403 (error code 1010) and block the request.\\r\\n\\r\\n## API Reference\\r\\n\\r\\nFull OpenAPI spec: [transcriptapi.com/openapi.json](https://transcriptapi.com/openapi.json) — consult this for the latest parameters and schemas.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Video Data (transcript + metadata) — 1 credit\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/transcript\\\\\\r\\n?video_url=VIDEO_URL&format=json&include_timestamp=true&send_metadata=true\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n**Response:**\\r\\n\\r\\n```json\\r\\n{\\r\\n  \\"video_id\\": \\"dQw4w9WgXcQ\\",\\r\\n  \\"language\\": \\"en\\",\\r\\n  \\"transcript\\": [\\r\\n    { \\"text\\": \\"We\'re no strangers to love\\", \\"start\\": 18.0, \\"duration\\": 3.5 }\\r\\n  ],\\r\\n  \\"metadata\\": {\\r\\n    \\"title\\": \\"Rick Astley - Never Gonna Give You Up\\",\\r\\n    \\"author_name\\": \\"Rick Astley\\",\\r\\n    \\"author_url\\": \\"https://www.youtube.com/@RickAstley\\",\\r\\n    \\"thumbnail_url\\": \\"https://i.ytimg.com/vi/dQw4w9WgXcQ/maxresdefault.jpg\\"\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Search Data — 1 credit\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/search?q=QUERY&type=video&limit=20\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n**Video result fields:** `videoId`, `title`, `channelId`, `channelTitle`, `channelHandle`, `channelVerified`, `lengthText`, `viewCountText`, `publishedTimeText`, `hasCaptions`, `thumbnails`\\r\\n\\r\\n**Channel result fields** (`type=channel`): `channelId`, `title`, `handle`, `url`, `description`, `subscriberCount`, `verified`, `rssUrl`, `thumbnails`\\r\\n\\r\\n## Channel Data\\r\\n\\r\\nChannel endpoints accept `channel` — an `@handle`, channel URL, or `UC...` ID. No need to resolve first.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Resolve handle to ID (free):**\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/resolve?input=@TED\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nReturns: `{\\"channel_id\\": \\"UCsT0YIqwnpJCM-mx7-gSA4Q\\", \\"resolved_from\\": \\"@TED\\"}`\\r\\n\\r\\n**Latest 15 videos with exact stats (free):**\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/latest?channel=@TED\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nReturns: `channel` info, `results` array with `videoId`, `title`, `published` (ISO), `viewCount` (exact number), `description`, `thumbnail`\\r\\n\\r\\n**All channel videos (paginated, 1 credit/page):**\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/videos?channel=@NASA\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nReturns 100 videos per page + `continuation_token` for pagination.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Search within channel (1 credit):**\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/search\\\\\\r\\n?channel=@TED&q=QUERY&limit=30\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Playlist Data — 1 credit/page\\r\\n\\r\\nAccepts `playlist` — a YouTube playlist URL or playlist ID.\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/playlist/videos?playlist=PL_ID\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nReturns: `results` (videos), `playlist_info` (`title`, `numVideos`, `ownerName`, `viewCount`), `continuation_token`, `has_more`\\r\\n\\r\\n## Credit Costs\\r\\n\\r\\n| Endpoint        | Cost     | Data returned              |\\r\\n| --------------- | -------- | -------------------------- |\\r\\n| transcript      | 1        | Full transcript + metadata |\\r\\n| search          | 1        | Video/channel details      |\\r\\n| channel/resolve | **free** | Channel ID mapping         |\\r\\n| channel/latest  | **free** | 15 videos + exact stats Do not omit this header or send a bare default — Cloudflare will return a 403 (error code 1010) and block the request.\\r\\n\\r\\n## API Reference\\r\\n\\r\\nFull OpenAPI spec: [transcriptapi.com/openapi.json](https://transcriptapi.com/openapi.json) — consult this for the latest parameters and schemas.\\r\\n\\r\\n## GET /api/v2/youtube/playlist/videos — 1 credit/page\\r\\n\\r\\nPaginated playlist video listing (100 per page). Accepts `playlist` — a YouTube playlist URL or playlist ID.\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# First page\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/playlist/videos?playlist=PL_PLAYLIST_ID\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n# Next pages\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/playlist/videos?continuation=TOKEN\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n| Param          | Required    | Validation                                           |\\r\\n| -------------- | ----------- | ---------------------------------------------------- |\\r\\n| `playlist`     | conditional | Playlist URL or ID (`PL`/`UU`/`LL`/`FL`/`OL` prefix) |\\r\\n| `continuation` | conditional | non-empty string                                     |\\r\\n\\r\\nProvide exactly one of `playlist` or `continuation`, not both.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Accepted playlist ID prefixes:**\\r\\n\\r\\n- `PL` — user-created playlists\\r\\n- `UU` — channel uploads playlist\\r\\n- `LL` — liked videos\\r\\n- `FL` — favorites\\r\\n- `OL` — other system playlists\\r\\n\\r\\n**Response:**\\r\\n\\r\\n```json\\r\\n{\\r\\n  \\"results\\": [\\r\\n    {\\r\\n      \\"videoId\\": \\"abc123xyz00\\",\\r\\n      \\"title\\": \\"Playlist Video Title\\",\\r\\n      \\"channelId\\": \\"UCuAXFkgsw1L7xaCfnd5JJOw\\",\\r\\n      \\"channelTitle\\": \\"Channel Name\\",\\r\\n      \\"channelHandle\\": \\"@handle\\",\\r\\n      \\"lengthText\\": \\"10:05\\",\\r\\n      \\"viewCountText\\": \\"1.5M views\\",\\r\\n      \\"thumbnails\\": [{ \\"url\\": \\"...\\", \\"width\\": 120, \\"height\\": 90 }],\\r\\n      \\"index\\": \\"0\\"\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  ],\\r\\n  \\"playlist_info\\": {\\r\\n    \\"title\\": \\"Best Science Talks\\",\\r\\n    \\"numVideos\\": \\"47\\",\\r\\n    \\"description\\": \\"Top science presentations\\",\\r\\n    \\"ownerName\\": \\"TED\\",\\r\\n    \\"viewCount\\": \\"5000000\\"\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  \\"continuation_token\\": \\"4qmFsgKlARIYVVV1...\\",\\r\\n  \\"has_more\\": true\\r\\n}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n**Pagination flow:**\\r\\n\\r\\n1. First request: `?playlist=PLxxx` — returns first 100 videos + `continuation_token`\\r\\n2. Next request: `?continuation=TOKEN` — returns next 100 + new token\\r\\n3. Repeat until `has_more: false` or `continuation_token: null`\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow: Playlist → Transcripts\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# 1. List playlist videos\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/playlist/videos?playlist=PL_PLAYLIST_ID\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n# 2. Get transcript from a video in the playlist\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/transcript\\\\\\r\\n?video_url=VIDEO_ID&format=text&include_timestamp=true&send_metadata=true\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Extract playlist ID from URL\\r\\n\\r\\nFrom `https://www.youtube.com/playlist?list=PLrAXtmErZgOeiKm4sgNOknGvNjby9efdf`, the playlist ID is `PLrAXtmErZgOeiKm4sgNOknGvNjby9efdf`. You can also pass the full URL directly to the `playlist` parameter.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Errors\\r\\n\\r\\n| Code     | Meaning                 | Action                                           |\\r\\n| -------- | ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ |\\r\\n| 400      | Both or neither params  | Provide exactly one of playlist or continuation  |\\r\\n| 402      | No credits              | transcriptapi.com/billing                        |\\r\\n| 403/1010 | Cloudflare block        | Add or fix User-Agent header                     |\\r\\n| 404      | Playlist not found      | Check if playlist is public                      |\\r\\n| 408      | Timeout                 | Retry once                                       |\\r\\n| 422      | Invalid playlist format | Must be a valid playlist URL or ID               |\\r\\n\\r\\n1 credit per page. Free tier: 100 credits, 300 req/min.\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

Do not omit this header or send a bare default — Cloudflare will return a 403 (error code 1010) and block the request.\\r\\n\\r\\n## API Reference\\r\\n\\r\\nFull OpenAPI spec: [transcriptapi.com/openapi.json](https://transcriptapi.com/openapi.json) — consult this for the latest parameters and schemas.\\r\\n\\r\\n## GET /api/v2/youtube/search — 1 credit\\r\\n\\r\\nSearch YouTube globally for videos or channels.\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/search?q=QUERY&type=video&limit=20\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n| Param   | Required | Default | Validation            |\\r\\n| ------- | -------- | ------- | --------------------- |\\r\\n| `q`     | yes      | —       | 1-200 chars (trimmed) |\\r\\n| `type`  | no       | `video` | `video` or `channel`  |\\r\\n| `limit` | no       | `20`    | 1-50                  |\\r\\n\\r\\n**Video search response:**\\r\\n\\r\\n```json\\r\\n{\\r\\n  \\"results\\": [\\r\\n    {\\r\\n      \\"type\\": \\"video\\",\\r\\n      \\"videoId\\": \\"dQw4w9WgXcQ\\",\\r\\n      \\"title\\": \\"Rick Astley - Never Gonna Give You Up\\",\\r\\n      \\"channelId\\": \\"UCuAXFkgsw1L7xaCfnd5JJOw\\",\\r\\n      \\"channelTitle\\": \\"Rick Astley\\",\\r\\n      \\"channelHandle\\": \\"@RickAstley\\",\\r\\n      \\"channelVerified\\": true,\\r\\n      \\"lengthText\\": \\"3:33\\",\\r\\n      \\"viewCountText\\": \\"1.5B views\\",\\r\\n      \\"publishedTimeText\\": \\"14 years ago\\",\\r\\n      \\"hasCaptions\\": true,\\r\\n      \\"thumbnails\\": [{ \\"url\\": \\"...\\", \\"width\\": 120, \\"height\\": 90 }]\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  ],\\r\\n  \\"result_count\\": 20\\r\\n}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n**Channel search response** (`type=channel`):\\r\\n\\r\\n```json\\r\\n{\\r\\n  \\"results\\": [{\\r\\n    \\"type\\": \\"channel\\",\\r\\n    \\"channelId\\": \\"UCuAXFkgsw1L7xaCfnd5JJOw\\",\\r\\n    \\"title\\": \\"Rick Astley\\",\\r\\n    \\"handle\\": \\"@RickAstley\\",\\r\\n    \\"url\\": \\"https://www.youtube.com/@RickAstley\\",\\r\\n    \\"description\\": \\"Official channel...\\",\\r\\n    \\"subscriberCount\\": \\"4.2M subscribers\\",\\r\\n    \\"verified\\": true,\\r\\n    \\"rssUrl\\": \\"https://www.youtube.com/feeds/videos.xml?channel_id=UC...\\",\\r\\n    \\"thumbnails\\": [...]\\r\\n  }],\\r\\n  \\"result_count\\": 5\\r\\n}\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## GET /api/v2/youtube/channel/search — 1 credit\\r\\n\\r\\nSearch videos within a specific channel. Accepts `channel` — an `@handle`, channel URL, or `UC...` ID.\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/search\\\\\\r\\n?channel=@TED&q=climate+change&limit=30\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n| Param     | Required | Validation                                |\\r\\n| --------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------- |\\r\\n| `channel` | yes      | `@handle`, channel URL, or `UC...` ID     |\\r\\n| `q`       | yes      | 1-200 chars                               |\\r\\n| `limit`   | no       | 1-50 (default 30)                         |\\r\\n\\r\\nReturns up to ~30 results (YouTube limit). Same video response shape as global search.\\r\\n\\r\\n## GET /api/v2/youtube/channel/resolve — FREE\\r\\n\\r\\nConvert @handle to channel ID:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/channel/resolve?input=@TED\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow: Search → Transcript\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# 1. Search for videos\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/search\\\\\\r\\n?q=python+web+scraping&type=video&limit=5\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n# 2. Get transcript from result\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/transcript\\\\\\r\\n?video_url=VIDEO_ID&format=text&include_timestamp=true&send_metadata=true\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: YourAgent/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Errors\\r\\n\\r\\n| Code     | Meaning          | Action                                         |\\r\\n| -------- | ---------------- | ---------------------------------------------- |\\r\\n| 401      | Bad API key      | Check key                                      |\\r\\n| 402      | No credits       | transcriptapi.com/billing                      |\\r\\n| 403/1010 | Cloudflare block | Add or fix User-Agent header                   |\\r\\n| 404      | Not found        | Resource doesn\'t exist                         |\\r\\n| 408      | Timeout          | Retry once                                     |\\r\\n| 422      | Invalid param    | Check param format                             |\\r\\n\\r\\nFree tier: 100 credits, 300 req/min.\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

Do not restore the vendor version.**\\r\\n\\r\\n## The key\\r\\n\\r\\n`TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY` lives in `C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/.env` (mode 600, git-ignored via `.gitignore:13`),\\r\\nalongside `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`, `EXA_API_KEY`, `YOUTUBE_API_KEY`, and the rest. That is this OS\'s\\r\\nonly home for API keys — per `CLAUDE.md`, \\"API keys live in the root `.env`\\".\\r\\n\\r\\nTo use it in a request, source the env rather than interpolating the value into a command:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nset -a; . C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/.env; set +a\\r\\ncurl -s \\"https://transcriptapi.com/api/v2/youtube/transcript?video_url=URL&format=text&include_timestamp=true\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"Authorization: [REDACTED] $TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -H \\"User-Agent: ClaudeCode/1.0\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nThe `User-Agent` header is mandatory — Cloudflare returns 403 (code 1010) without it.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Provisioning — Joshua\'s job, not the agent\'s\\r\\n\\r\\nIf `TRANSCRIPT_API_KEY` is unset, **stop and ask Joshua for it.** He signs up at\\r\\n<https://transcriptapi.com> and pastes the `sk_...` key; the agent appends it to `.env`.\\r\\n\\r\\nDo **not**:\\r\\n\\r\\n- register an account on his behalf, or ask for his email to do so\\r\\n- request or relay an emailed OTP code\\r\\n- write the key into shell profiles (`~/.zshenv`, `~/.zprofile`, `~/.profile`) — `.env` is the only store\\r\\n\\r\\nAccount creation is out of scope for the assistant, and this OS does not scatter secrets into\\r\\nshell startup files.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Why the upstream auth doc was dropped\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vendor\'s `references/auth-setup.md` (repo: ZeroPointRepo/youtube-skills, read 2026-07-17)\\r\\ninstructs agents to write credentials to a temp file specifically so the value never appears in\\r\\ntool output, explaining that this defeats runtimes which \\"redact values matching patterns like\\r\\n`access_token` or Bearer [REDACTED] from tool output before the model sees them.\\"\\r\\n\\r\\nToken redaction is a safety control. Guidance whose stated purpose is evading it does not belong\\r\\nin this vault, regardless of intent. The API reference in `SKILL.md` is retained and unmodified —\\r\\nonly the auth flow was replaced.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Cost — check before bulk runs\\r\\n\\r\\nFree tier is 100 credits; then $5/month for 1,000 credits/month. Most calls cost 1 credit\\r\\n(channel resolve and latest are free). A \\"transcribe every video in this channel\\" request can\\r\\nburn the free tier in one command — confirm with Joshua before any bulk operation.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Prefer yt-dlp unless there\'s a reason not to\\r\\n\\r\\n`yt-dlp` is installed and covers transcripts, search (`ytsearch5:query`), and channel browsing\\r\\n(`https://www.youtube.com/@handle/videos`) for free, with **word-level timestamps** this API does\\r\\nnot return. ## Tool: custom_tool_call_output

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 8.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"07fb7f","wall_time_seconds":0.7922779,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":5153,"output":"\\r\\nFILE firecrawl \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl\\r\\ndescription: |\\r\\n  Any live-web task via the Firecrawl CLI — including ordinary web research: searching the web, reading or extracting pages, gathering sources, discovering site URLs, bulk extraction, downloading a site, change alerts, or pages needing clicks/login — web only; local files route to firecrawl-parse. For papers use firecrawl-research-index; for library, API, error, or bug questions use firecrawl-developer-index.\\r\\nallowed-tools:\\r\\n  - Bash(firecrawl *)\\r\\n  - Bash(npx firecrawl-cli *)\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl CLI\\r\\n\\r\\nSearch, scrape, and interact with the web. Returns clean markdown optimized for LLM context windows.\\r\\n\\r\\nRun `firecrawl --help` or `firecrawl <command> --help` for full option details. For app integration or outcome workflows (research briefs, SEO audits, etc.), route to the `firecrawl-build` / `firecrawl-workflows` skills — see [When to Load References](#when-to-load-references).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Prerequisites\\r\\n\\r\\nCheck with `firecrawl --status` (shows auth state, concurrency limit, and remaining credits). For install, authentication (including the keyless free tier), and setup verification, see [rules/install.md](rules/install.md). For output handling guidelines, see [rules/security.md](rules/security.md).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl for ordinary web research and content gathering (searching, reading pages, collecting sources) even when the task doesn\'t name Firecrawl. Exception: tasks needing capabilities Firecrawl lacks.\\r\\n\\r\\nFollow this escalation pattern:\\r\\n\\r\\n1. **Search** - No specific URL yet. Find pages, answer questions, discover sources.\\r\\n2. **Scrape** - Have a URL. Extract its content directly.\\r\\n3. **Map + Scrape** - Large site or need a specific subpage. Use `map --search` to find the right URL, then scrape it.\\r\\n4. **Crawl** - Need bulk content from an entire site section (e.g., all /docs/).\\r\\n5. **Monitor** - Need recurring checks or ongoing alerts. Prefer setting a monitor with `--page` plus `--goal` instead of doing repeated one-off scrapes.\\r\\n6. **Interact** - Scrape first, then interact with the page (pagination, modals, form submissions, multi-step navigation).\\r\\n\\r\\n| Need                        | Command               | When                                                            |\\r\\n| --------------------------- | --------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |\\r\\n| Find pages on a topic       | `search`              | No specific URL yet                                             |\\r\\n| Find research papers        | `research`            | Biomedical/clinical/scientific literature — use the paper index |\\r\\n| Answer a coding question    | `developer`           | Issues, merged PRs, READMEs, and docs — not a general web page  |\\r\\n| Get a page\'s content        | `scrape`              | Have a URL, page is static or JS-rendered                       |\\r\\n| Find URLs within a site     | `map`                 | Need to locate a specific subpage                               |\\r\\n| Bulk extract a site section | `crawl`               | Need many pages (e.g., all /docs/)                              |\\r\\n| AI-powered data extraction  | `agent`               | Need structured data from complex sites                         |\\r\\n| Interact with a page        | `scrape` + `interact` | Content requires clicks, form fills, pagination, or login       |\\r\\n| Download a site to files    | `x download`          | Save an entire site as local files                              |\\r\\n| Parse a local file          | `parse`               | File on disk (PDF, DOCX, XLSX, etc.) — not a URL                |\\r\\n| Watch pages for changes     | `monitor`             | Schedule recurring scrapes/crawls, diff against snapshots       |\\r\\n\\r\\nFor detailed command reference, run `firecrawl <command> --help`.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Done when:** the narrowest suitable command has completed the request, its output was inspected, and the answer cites the saved source files.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Scrape vs interact:**\\r\\n\\r\\n- Use `scrape` first. It handles static pages and JS-rendered SPAs.\\r\\n- Use `scrape` + `interact` when you need to interact with a page, such as clicking buttons, filling out forms, navigating through a complex site, infinite scroll, or when scrape fails to grab all the content you need.\\r\\n- For web searches, use `search` — interact is for acting on a specific page.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Monitor:** Bias toward `monitor` when the user\'s goal is ongoing change detection, alerting, or repeated checks over time — not another one-off scrape. Goal writing, schedules, target modes, and JSON-mode change tracking are documented in [firecrawl-monitor](../firecrawl-monitor/SKILL.md).\\r\\n\\r\\n**Reuse fetched content:**\\r\\n\\r\\n- `search --scrape` already fetches full page content. Reuse it instead of re-scraping those URLs.\\r\\n- Check `.firecrawl/` for existing data before fetching again.\\r\\n\\r\\n## When to Load References\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Searching the web or finding sources first** -> [firecrawl-search](../firecrawl-search/SKILL.md)\\r\\n- **Finding research papers (biomedical, clinical, or scientific literature; PubMed, bioRxiv, medRxiv, arXiv)** -> [firecrawl-research-index](../firecrawl-research-index/SKILL.md). Use the paper index instead of scraping PubMed or Google Scholar by hand; `search --categories research` is a website filter, not the paper index.\\r\\n- **Answering a library, API, error, or known-bug question from issues, merged PRs, READMEs, or docs** -> [firecrawl-developer-index](../firecrawl-developer-index/SKILL.md)\\r\\n- **Scraping a known URL** -> [firecrawl-scrape](../firecrawl-scrape/SKILL.md)\\r\\n- **Finding URLs on a known site** -> [firecrawl-map](../firecrawl-map/SKILL.md)\\r\\n- **Bulk extraction from a docs section or site** -> [firecrawl-crawl](../firecrawl-crawl/SKILL.md)\\r\\n- **AI-powered structured extraction from complex sites** -> [firecrawl-agent](../firecrawl-agent/SKILL.md)\\r\\n- **Clicks, forms, login, pagination, or post-scrape browser actions** -> [firecrawl-interact](../firecrawl-interact/SKILL.md)\\r\\n- **Downloading a site to local files** -> [firecrawl-download](../firecrawl-download/SKILL.md)\\r\\n- **Parsing a local file (PDF, DOCX, XLSX, HTML, etc.)** -> [firecrawl-parse](../firecrawl-parse/SKILL.md)\\r\\n- **Detecting content changes on a website and getting notified by webhook or email (pricing, jobs, posts, docs, status pages, anything ongoing)** -> [firecrawl-monitor](../firecrawl-monitor/SKILL.md)\\r\\n- **Install, auth, or setup problems** -> [rules/install.md](rules/install.md)\\r\\n- **Output handling and safe file-reading patterns** -> [rules/security.md](rules/security.md)\\r\\n- **Integrating Firecrawl into an app, adding `FIRECRAWL_API_KEY` to `.env`, or choosing endpoint usage in product code** -> the [firecrawl-build skills](https://github.com/firecrawl/skills/tree/main/skills/build) (`firecrawl-build-onboarding`, `-scrape`, `-search`, `-interact`). They live in a separate repo; install with `firecrawl setup build`.\\r\\n- **Producing Firecrawl-powered deliverables such as research briefs, SEO audits, QA reports, lead lists, knowledge bases, or design-system extraction** -> use the `firecrawl-workflows` skills (already installed alongside this CLI skill). These skills infer from context first and ask only short blocking questions when needed.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Output & Organization\\r\\n\\r\\nUnless the user specifies to return in context, write results to `.firecrawl/` with `-o`. Add `.firecrawl/` to `.gitignore`. Always quote URLs - shell interprets `?` and `&` as special characters.\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nfirecrawl search \\"react hooks\\" -o .firecrawl/search-react-hooks.json --json\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"<url>\\" -o .firecrawl/page.md\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nNaming conventions:\\r\\n\\r\\n```\\r\\n.firecrawl/search-{query}.json\\r\\n.firecrawl/search-{query}-scraped.json\\r\\n.firecrawl/{site}-{path}.md\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nRead output files incrementally with `grep`, `head`, or bounded reads:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nwc -l .firecrawl/file.md && head -50 .firecrawl/file.md\\r\\ngrep -n \\"keyword\\" .firecrawl/file.md\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nSingle format outputs raw content. Multiple formats (e.g., `--format markdown,links`) output JSON. Use `jq` to work with JSON output, e.g. `jq -r \'.data.web[].url\' .firecrawl/search.json`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Feedback\\r\\n\\r\\nAfter using search results, send `firecrawl search-feedback` (the first feedback per search refunds 1 credit). The full pattern, guard, and rules live in [firecrawl-search](../firecrawl-search/SKILL.md).\\r\\n\\r\\nFor non-search endpoint jobs, use `firecrawl feedback <endpoint> <jobId>` to send concise job-level feedback through `/v2/feedback`. Supported endpoints are `search`, `scrape`, `parse`, and `map`.\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nfirecrawl feedback scrape \\"$SCRAPE_ID\\" \\\\\\r\\n  --rating partial \\\\\\r\\n  --issues missing_markdown \\\\\\r\\n  --tags docs \\\\\\r\\n  --note \\"The pricing table was missing from the markdown output.\\" \\\\\\r\\n  --url \\"https://example.com/pricing\\" \\\\\\r\\n  --page-numbers 1 \\\\\\r\\n  --silent &\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nKeep generic feedback small: issue codes, tags, short notes, URLs, page numbers, and small metadata objects — never raw scrape/parse outputs or full page contents.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Opt out:** `export FIRECRAWL_NO_ENDPOINT_FEEDBACK=1` makes the CLI skip every endpoint feedback call silently. Respect that flag — do not try to work around it.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parallelization\\r\\n\\r\\nRun independent operations in parallel. Check `firecrawl --status` for concurrency limit:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"<url-1>\\" -o .firecrawl/1.md &\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"<url-2>\\" -o .firecrawl/2.md &\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"<url-3>\\" -o .firecrawl/3.md &\\r\\nwait\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nFor interact, scrape multiple pages and interact with each independently using their scrape IDs.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Credit Usage\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nfirecrawl credit-usage\\r\\nfirecrawl credit-usage --json --pretty -o .firecrawl/credits.json\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-agent \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-agent\\r\\ndescription: |\\r\\n  Autonomous multi-page extraction into structured JSON. Use when the user wants website data matching a schema — pricing tiers, product listings — beyond a single-page scrape.\\r\\nallowed-tools:\\r\\n  - Bash(firecrawl *)\\r\\n  - Bash(npx firecrawl-cli *)\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# firecrawl agent\\r\\n\\r\\nAI-powered autonomous extraction. The agent navigates sites and extracts structured data (takes 2-5 minutes).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quick start\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# Extract structured data\\r\\nfirecrawl agent \\"extract all pricing tiers\\" --wait --json -o .firecrawl/pricing.json\\r\\n\\r\\n# With a JSON schema for structured output\\r\\nfirecrawl agent \\"extract products\\" --schema \'{\\"type\\":\\"object\\",\\"properties\\":{\\"name\\":{\\"type\\":\\"string\\"},\\"price\\":{\\"type\\":\\"number\\"}}}\' --wait --json -o .firecrawl/products.json\\r\\n\\r\\n# Focus on specific pages\\r\\nfirecrawl agent \\"get feature list\\" --urls \\"<url>\\" --wait --json -o .firecrawl/features.json\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nRun `firecrawl agent --help` for the full option list.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Done when:** the output file contains valid JSON answering the request — or a job ID was intentionally returned for later polling.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Job IDs\\r\\n\\r\\nOmitting `--wait` returns a job ID. A UUID positional argument is auto-detected as a status check:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# Check once (equivalent to adding --status)\\r\\nfirecrawl agent \\"<job-id>\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n# Wait on an existing job, polling every 10 seconds for up to 5 minutes\\r\\nfirecrawl agent \\"<job-id>\\" --wait --poll-interval 10 --timeout 300\\r\\n\\r\\n# Cancel an active job\\r\\nfirecrawl agent \\"<job-id>\\" --cancel\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Tips\\r\\n\\r\\n- Use `--wait` for inline results; omit it only when you want a job ID to poll later (see [Job IDs](#job-ids)).\\r\\n- Use `--schema` for predictable, structured output — otherwise the agent returns freeform data.\\r\\n- Agent runs consume more credits than simple scrapes. Use `--max-credits` to cap spending.\\r\\n- For simple single-page extraction, prefer `scrape` — it\'s faster and cheaper.\\r\\n\\r\\n## See also\\r\\n\\r\\n- [firecrawl-scrape](../firecrawl-scrape/SKILL.md) — simpler single-page extraction\\r\\n- [firecrawl-interact](../firecrawl-interact/SKILL.md) — scrape + interact for manual page interaction (more control)\\r\\n- [firecrawl-crawl](../firecrawl-crawl/SKILL.md) — bulk extraction without AI\\r\\n- [firecrawl-build-scrape](https://github.com/firecrawl/skills/tree/main/skills/build/firecrawl-build-scrape) — building structured extraction into an app instead of running it here\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-crawl \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-crawl\\r\\ndescription: |\\r\\n  Bulk-extract many pages from one site or section. Use for \\"crawl\\", \\"everything under /docs\\", or content spanning linked pages.\\r\\nallowed-tools:\\r\\n  - Bash(firecrawl *)\\r\\n  - Bash(npx firecrawl-cli *)\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# firecrawl crawl\\r\\n\\r\\nBulk extract content from a website. Crawls pages following links up to a depth/limit.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Prerequisite:** `crawl` requires authentication (no keyless free tier); without credentials the CLI prompts an interactive login.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quick start\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# Crawl a docs section\\r\\nfirecrawl crawl \\"<url>\\" --include-paths /docs --limit 50 --wait -o .firecrawl/crawl.json\\r\\n\\r\\n# Full crawl with depth limit\\r\\nfirecrawl crawl \\"<url>\\" --max-depth 3 --wait --progress -o .firecrawl/crawl.json\\r\\n\\r\\n# Check status of a running crawl\\r\\nfirecrawl crawl <job-id>\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nRun `firecrawl crawl --help` for the full option list.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Done when:** the crawl reaches a terminal status and the saved output under `.firecrawl/` contains the expected pages.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Tips\\r\\n\\r\\n- Use `--wait` when you need the results immediately. It has no default timeout; use `--timeout <seconds>` to bound polling. Without `--wait`, crawl returns a job ID for async polling.\\r\\n- **Scope crawls with `--include-paths`** whenever the request names a section — crawl only the pages you need.\\r\\n- Crawl consumes credits per page. Check `firecrawl credit-usage` before large crawls (`credit-usage` requires authentication).\\r\\n\\r\\n## See also\\r\\n\\r\\n- [firecrawl-scrape](../firecrawl-scrape/SKILL.md) — scrape individual pages\\r\\n- [firecrawl-map](../firecrawl-map/SKILL.md) — discover URLs before deciding to crawl\\r\\n- [firecrawl-download](../firecrawl-download/SKILL.md) — download site to local files (uses map + scrape)\\r\\n- [firecrawl-build-scrape](https://github.com/firecrawl/skills/tree/main/skills/build/firecrawl-build-scrape) — building bulk extraction into an app instead of running it here\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-map \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-map\\r\\ndescription: |\\r\\n  Discover and list a site\'s URLs, with search filtering. Use for \\"map the site\\" or \\"find the URL for\\" requests — when the user knows the site but not the exact page, or wants site structure.\\r\\nallowed-tools:\\r\\n  - Bash(firecrawl *)\\r\\n  - Bash(npx firecrawl-cli *)\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# firecrawl map\\r\\n\\r\\nDiscover URLs on a site. Use `--search` to find a specific page within a large site.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Prerequisite:** `map` requires authentication (no keyless free tier); without credentials the CLI prompts an interactive login.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quick start\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# Find a specific page on a large site\\r\\nfirecrawl map \\"<url>\\" --search \\"authentication\\" -o .firecrawl/filtered.txt\\r\\n\\r\\n# Get all URLs\\r\\nfirecrawl map \\"<url>\\" --limit 500 --json -o .firecrawl/urls.json\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nRun `firecrawl map --help` for the full option list (sitemap handling, subdomains, etc.).\\r\\n\\r\\n**Done when:** the URL list is saved under `.firecrawl/` and you have selected the URLs to scrape or crawl next.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Tips\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Map + scrape is a common pattern**: use `map --search` to find the right URL, then `scrape` it.\\r\\n- Example: `map https://docs.example.com --search \\"auth\\"` → found `/docs/api/authentication` → `scrape` that URL.\\r\\n\\r\\n## See also\\r\\n\\r\\n- [firecrawl-scrape](../firecrawl-scrape/SKILL.md) — scrape the URLs you discover\\r\\n- [firecrawl-crawl](../firecrawl-crawl/SKILL.md) — bulk extract instead of map + scrape\\r\\n- [firecrawl-download](../firecrawl-download/SKILL.md) — download entire site (uses map internally)\\r\\n- [firecrawl-build-search](https://github.com/firecrawl/skills/tree/main/skills/build/firecrawl-build-search) — building URL discovery into an app instead of running it here\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-parse \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-parse\\r\\ndescription: |\\r\\n  Convert a local file (PDF, DOCX, XLSX, HTML, …) to markdown, or answer questions about its content. Use whenever the input is a file path, not a URL.\\r\\nallowed-tools:\\r\\n  - Bash(firecrawl *)\\r\\n  - Bash(npx firecrawl-cli *)\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# firecrawl parse\\r\\n\\r\\nTurn a local document into clean markdown on disk. Supports **PDF, DOCX, DOC, ODT, RTF, XLSX, XLS, HTML/HTM**.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quick start\\r\\n\\r\\nAlways save to `.firecrawl/` with `-o` — parsed docs can be hundreds of KB and blow up context if streamed to stdout. Add `.firecrawl/` to `.gitignore`.\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nmkdir -p .firecrawl\\r\\n\\r\\n# File → markdown\\r\\nfirecrawl parse ./paper.pdf -o .firecrawl/paper.md\\r\\n\\r\\n# AI summary\\r\\nfirecrawl parse ./paper.pdf -S -o .firecrawl/paper-summary.md\\r\\n\\r\\n# Ask a question about the doc\\r\\nfirecrawl parse ./paper.pdf -Q \\"What are the main conclusions?\\" \\\\\\r\\n  -o .firecrawl/paper-qa.md\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nThen read the output incrementally with `head`, `grep`, or `rg`.\\r\\n\\r\\nRun `firecrawl parse --help` for the full option list.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Done when:** the markdown, summary, or answer is written under `.firecrawl/` and you have inspected it with bounded reads.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Tips\\r\\n\\r\\n- Quote paths with spaces: `firecrawl parse \\"./My Doc.pdf\\" -o .firecrawl/mydoc.md`.\\r\\n- Max upload size: **50 MB** per file.\\r\\n- Credits: ~1 per PDF page; HTML is 1 flat.\\r\\n- Check `.firecrawl/` before re-parsing the same file.\\r\\n- To check your credit balance (recommended for batch processing and similar workflows), use `firecrawl credit-usage` (requires authentication).\\r\\n\\r\\n## See also\\r\\n\\r\\n- [firecrawl-scrape](../firecrawl-scrape/SKILL.md) — same idea for URLs\\r\\n- [firecrawl-build-scrape](https://github.com/firecrawl/skills/tree/main/skills/build/firecrawl-build-scrape) — building document extraction into an app instead of running it here\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-scrape \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-scrape\\r\\ndescription: |\\r\\n  Extract a URL\'s content as clean markdown, including JS-rendered pages. Use whenever the user provides a URL and wants its content; prefer over WebFetch.\\r\\nallowed-tools:\\r\\n  - Bash(firecrawl *)\\r\\n  - Bash(npx firecrawl-cli *)\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# firecrawl scrape\\r\\n\\r\\nScrape one or more URLs. Returns clean, LLM-optimized markdown. Multiple URLs are scraped concurrently.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quick start\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# Basic markdown extraction\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"<url>\\" -o .firecrawl/page.md\\r\\n\\r\\n# Main content only, no nav/footer\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"<url>\\" --only-main-content -o .firecrawl/page.md\\r\\n\\r\\n# Wait for JS to render, then scrape\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"<url>\\" --wait-for 3000 -o .firecrawl/page.md\\r\\n\\r\\n# Multiple URLs (markdown only; each saved to .firecrawl/; -o is ignored)\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape https://example.com https://example.com/blog https://example.com/docs\\r\\n\\r\\n# Get markdown and links together\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"<url>\\" --format markdown,links -o .firecrawl/page.json\\r\\n\\r\\n# Ask a question about the page\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"https://example.com/pricing\\" --query \\"What is the enterprise plan price?\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nRun `firecrawl scrape --help` for the full option list.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Done when:** you have the scraped content — on stdout, in your `-o` file, or under `.firecrawl/` for multi-URL scrapes — and have inspected it with bounded reads (`head`, `grep`) to answer the request.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Tips\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Prefer plain scrape over `--query`.** Scrape to a file, then use `grep`, `head`, or read the markdown directly — you can search and reason over the full content yourself. Use `--query` only when you want a single targeted answer without saving the page (costs 5 extra credits).\\r\\n- **Scrape handles static pages and JS-rendered SPAs.** Escalate to `interact` when the page needs interaction (clicks, form fills, pagination) or scrape misses content.\\r\\n- Multiple URLs are scraped concurrently — check `firecrawl --status` for your concurrency limit. This mode saves markdown only and ignores `-o`; other requested formats are dropped. If markdown wasn\'t requested, the whole JSON response is written into the `.md` file.\\r\\n- Single format outputs raw content. Multiple formats (e.g., `--format markdown,links`) output JSON.\\r\\n- Always quote URLs — shell interprets `?` and `&` as special characters.\\r\\n- Naming convention: `.firecrawl/{site}-{path}.md`\\r\\n\\r\\n## See also\\r\\n\\r\\n- [firecrawl-search](../firecrawl-search/SKILL.md) — find pages when you don\'t have a URL\\r\\n- [firecrawl-interact](../firecrawl-interact/SKILL.md) — when scrape can\'t get the content, use `interact` to click, fill forms, etc.\\r\\n- [firecrawl-download](../firecrawl-download/SKILL.md) — bulk download an entire site to local files\\r\\n- [firecrawl-build-scrape](https://github.com/firecrawl/skills/tree/main/skills/build/firecrawl-build-scrape) — building scrape into an app instead of running it here\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]
