text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"python -X utf8 -c \"import json,pathlib;d=json.load(open('audits/skills-2026-10-09/global-review.json',encoding='utf-8'));names=['firecrawl-company-directories','firecrawl-competitive-intel','firecrawl-dashboard-reporting','firecrawl-demo-walkthrough','firecrawl-knowledge-base','firecrawl-knowledge-ingest','firecrawl-lead-gen','firecrawl-lead-research','firecrawl-market-research'];[(print('\\nFILE',n,'\\n',pathlib.Path(next(x['path'] for x in d['rows'] if x['name']==n)).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))) for n in names]\"",max_output_tokens:6500}));


## Tool: custom_tool_call_output

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 6.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"1b0668","wall_time_seconds":0.7982674,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":5376,"output":"\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-company-directories \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-company-directories\\r\\ndescription: Extract structured company lists from directories with Firecrawl. Use for scraping YC, Crunchbase, Product Hunt, G2, startup directories, category directories, or custom company databases into JSON, CSV, CRM-ready lists, or research tables.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Company Directories\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this to turn startup or company directories into structured lists.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer the directory, filters, result count, and output format from context. If the source is clear, proceed immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise questions only if blocked, such as the directory URL/name, required filters, or target result count.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl browser when the directory needs filters, pagination, infinite scroll, or profile clicks. Use scrape/map when listings are public and static.\\r\\n\\r\\nSuggested sources include YC companies, Crunchbase, Product Hunt, G2 categories, or any custom directory URL.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Extraction Fields\\r\\n\\r\\nCapture fields that are visible:\\r\\n\\r\\n- name\\r\\n- description\\r\\n- industry/category\\r\\n- stage/founded/location/team size/funding when visible\\r\\n- tags\\r\\n- directory profile URL\\r\\n- company website URL\\r\\n\\r\\nLeave unavailable fields blank. Do not infer.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# Company Directory Export: [Source]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Summary\\r\\n[Filters, count extracted, limitations]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Companies\\r\\n[Table or link to JSON/CSV]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Sources\\r\\n[Directory pages and profiles used]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-company-directories\\r\\ndirectory: [source]\\r\\nfilters: [criteria]\\r\\nmax_results: [number]\\r\\noutput: [json/csv/markdown]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## JSON Shape\\r\\n\\r\\nUse `source`, `filters`, `extractedAt`, `totalResults`, and `companies[]` with `name`, `url`, `description`, `industry`, `stage`, `founded`, `location`, `teamSize`, `funding`, `tags`, `profileUrl`, and `websiteUrl`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Deduplicate companies.\\r\\n- Track pagination progress.\\r\\n- Note rate limits, login walls, or CAPTCHA blocks.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-competitive-intel \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-competitive-intel\\r\\ndescription: Monitor competitor pricing, features, changelogs, dashboards, and product changes with Firecrawl. Use for recurring competitive intelligence, pricing tier extraction, feature change tracking, or structured competitor alerts.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Competitive Intel\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this for monitoring competitors over time. This is not the broad competitor analysis workflow.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer competitors, focus, cadence, and output format from context. If competitors are clear, proceed immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise questions only if blocked, such as the competitor list, focus area, or whether authenticated pages/profiles are required.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nFor each competitor, use Firecrawl scrape or browser as needed:\\r\\n\\r\\n- pricing pages, annual/monthly toggles, expanded feature tables\\r\\n- feature and product pages\\r\\n- changelogs, blogs, release notes, docs updates\\r\\n- authenticated dashboards only when the user has legitimate access\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parallel Work\\r\\n\\r\\nIf appropriate, use sub-agents or equivalent parallel task runners. A natural split is one competitor per researcher or one focus area per researcher.\\r\\n\\r\\nEach researcher should return pricing tiers, features, recent changes, source URLs, and confidence notes.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# Competitive Intel: [Competitors]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Alerts\\r\\n[Notable pricing, feature, or positioning changes]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Per-Competitor Breakdown\\r\\n[Pricing tiers, feature inventory, recent changes]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Cross-Competitor Comparison\\r\\n[Pricing table, feature matrix, key differentiators]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Suggested Follow-Ups\\r\\n[What to monitor next]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Sources\\r\\n[URLs visited]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-competitive-intel\\r\\ncompetitors: [list]\\r\\nfocus: [all/pricing/features/changelog]\\r\\ncadence: [one-off/weekly/monthly]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## JSON Shape\\r\\n\\r\\nWhen structured output is requested, include `generatedAt`, `competitors`, `pricing`, `recentChanges`, `features`, and `sources`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Extract real plan names, limits, and dates when available.\\r\\n- Note contact-sales or gated details instead of guessing.\\r\\n- Preserve sources for diffing future runs.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-dashboard-reporting \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-dashboard-reporting\\r\\ndescription: Pull metrics from analytics dashboards and internal web tools with Firecrawl browser. Use when the user needs dashboard reporting, cross-platform metric summaries, authenticated analytics extraction, date-range reports, or structured metrics from web dashboards.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Dashboard Reporting\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this to extract visible metrics from dashboards the user can legitimately access.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer dashboard URLs, metrics, date range, and output format from context. If dashboard targets are clear and accessible, proceed immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise questions only if blocked, such as the dashboard URLs, auth/profile requirement, or date range.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl browser for authenticated dashboards and UI interaction:\\r\\n\\r\\n- open each dashboard\\r\\n- set or verify date range\\r\\n- extract visible KPI cards, tables, and labels\\r\\n- click tabs, expand sections, and scroll tables\\r\\n- use export/download buttons only when appropriate and allowed\\r\\n\\r\\nIf login has expired, ask the user to re-authenticate rather than attempting to bypass access controls.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parallel Work\\r\\n\\r\\nIf appropriate, use sub-agents or equivalent parallel task runners. Split by dashboard platform or metric category. Each researcher should return metrics, units, period, source URL, and caveats.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# Dashboard Report\\r\\n\\r\\n## Summary\\r\\n[Highlights, alerts, trends]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Metrics By Dashboard\\r\\n[Platform, metric, value, unit, change, period]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Tables Or Exports\\r\\n[Captured tables/files and what they contain]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Notes And Caveats\\r\\n[Auth issues, chart-only data, unavailable metrics]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-dashboard-reporting\\r\\ndashboards: [urls]\\r\\ndate_range: [range]\\r\\nmetrics: [list]\\r\\noutput: [json/markdown]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## JSON Shape\\r\\n\\r\\nUse `reportedAt`, `dateRange`, `dashboards[]`, `metrics[]`, `tables[]`, `exports[]`, and `summary`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Extract actual numbers, not just chart labels.\\r\\n- Note when a chart cannot be read precisely.\\r\\n- Preserve date ranges and source URLs.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-demo-walkthrough \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-demo-walkthrough\\r\\ndescription: Walk through a product\'s key flows with Firecrawl browser and produce a structured UX/product walkthrough. Use for signup, onboarding, pricing, docs, dashboard, product demo prep, UX teardown, and first-run experience analysis.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Demo Walkthrough\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this to document a product experience step by step.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer the product URL, flow focus, and output format from context. If the URL is clear, proceed immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise questions only if blocked, such as the URL, desired flow focus, or credentials/constraints for protected areas.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl browser to open the product and navigate key flows. Snapshot at each step, scrape pages when useful, and document what the user sees and can do.\\r\\n\\r\\nDo not submit real credentials, purchases, or irreversible actions unless the user explicitly instructs and has permission.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parallel Work\\r\\n\\r\\nIf appropriate, use sub-agents or equivalent parallel task runners:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Homepage and Marketing\\r\\n- Signup and Onboarding\\r\\n- Pricing and Plans\\r\\n- Docs and Developer Experience\\r\\n- Dashboard and Core Product\\r\\n- Help and Support\\r\\n\\r\\nEach walker should return screens visited, actions taken, observations, friction, and source URLs.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# Product Walkthrough: [Product]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Product Overview\\r\\n[What the product does]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Flow Walkthroughs\\r\\n### [Flow Name]\\r\\n1. [Screen/Page] - what appears and what action is available\\r\\n2. [Next Screen] - what changes\\r\\n\\r\\n## Key Findings\\r\\n[First impression, standout patterns, friction points]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Recommendations\\r\\n[UX/product improvements]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Pages Visited\\r\\n[URLs]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-demo-walkthrough\\r\\nurl: [url]\\r\\nfocus: [full/signup/pricing/docs/dashboard]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Be specific about screens, CTAs, forms, and transitions.\\r\\n- Separate observation from opinion.\\r\\n- Preserve every page visited.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-knowledge-base \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-knowledge-base\\r\\ndescription: Build a knowledge base from web content with Firecrawl. Use for local reference docs, RAG-ready chunks, fine-tuning datasets, documentation mirrors, topic corpora, or LLM-ready markdown organized from web sources.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Knowledge Base\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this to turn URLs or topics into organized LLM-ready content.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer the source, goal, depth, and output location from context. If the source and goal are clear, proceed immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise questions only if blocked, such as the source URL/topic, whether the output is reference/RAG/training/docs, or training format if training is requested.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl map for documentation sites, search for topic-based corpora, scrape pages into markdown, and preserve code examples and tables.\\r\\n\\r\\nFor files, follow the Firecrawl download-style convention:\\r\\n\\r\\n```text\\r\\n.firecrawl/\\r\\n  <hostname>/\\r\\n    <path>/\\r\\n      index.md\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parallel Work\\r\\n\\r\\nIf appropriate, use sub-agents or equivalent parallel task runners:\\r\\n\\r\\n- one docs section per researcher\\r\\n- official docs, tutorials, community discussions, and references by source type\\r\\n- source scraping vs chunk generation vs manifest generation\\r\\n\\r\\n## Output Modes\\r\\n\\r\\n- Reference: markdown files, `index.md`, and `sources.json`.\\r\\n- RAG: markdown files plus chunk files and `manifest.json`.\\r\\n- Training: scraped source files plus `training-data.jsonl` and `training-metadata.json`.\\r\\n- Docs mirror: complete markdown mirror with a table of contents.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# Knowledge Base: [Source]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Summary\\r\\n[What was collected and why]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Output Structure\\r\\n[Files/directories created]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Coverage\\r\\n[Sections, source types, counts]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Usage Notes\\r\\n[How to use in RAG, docs, training, or agent context]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Sources\\r\\n[URLs collected]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-knowledge-base\\r\\nsource: [url/topic]\\r\\ngoal: [reference/rag/train/docs]\\r\\ndepth: [quick/thorough/exhaustive]\\r\\noutput_dir: [.firecrawl/]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Preserve code examples and formatting.\\r\\n- Remove boilerplate navigation where possible.\\r\\n- Include source URLs in frontmatter or metadata.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-knowledge-ingest \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-knowledge-ingest\\r\\ndescription: Ingest public or authenticated knowledge bases and docs portals with Firecrawl browser. Use for JS-heavy docs, login-gated portals, paginated help centers, support knowledge bases, or structured JSON/markdown extraction from documentation sites.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Knowledge Ingest\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this when a docs portal needs browser navigation, auth, pagination, or JS rendering.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer the portal URL, output format, auth needs, and page limit from context. If the portal is clear, proceed immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise questions only if blocked, such as the portal URL, whether authentication is required, or the desired output format.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl browser to:\\r\\n\\r\\n- open the portal and inspect navigation\\r\\n- identify sections, categories, sidebar links, and article URLs\\r\\n- follow sidebar navigation, next links, pagination, load-more controls, or search\\r\\n- scrape article content as markdown\\r\\n- extract metadata such as title, section, last updated date, author, and tags\\r\\n\\r\\nTry Firecrawl map as a supplement for public URLs, but use browser navigation for auth-gated or JS-heavy content.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# Knowledge Ingest: [Portal]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Summary\\r\\n[Pages extracted, sections covered, limitations]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Output\\r\\n[JSON/markdown/merged file path or content]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Sections\\r\\n[Section names and article counts]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Failed Or Restricted Pages\\r\\n[Any access/loading issues]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Sources\\r\\n[URLs extracted]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-knowledge-ingest\\r\\nurl: [portal url]\\r\\nformat: [json/markdown/merged]\\r\\nmax_pages: [number]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## JSON Shape\\r\\n\\r\\nUse `source`, `url`, `extractedAt`, `totalArticles`, and `sections[]` with article `title`, `url`, `section`, `content`, and `metadata`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Preserve code examples, tables, and formatting.\\r\\n- Strip nav chrome, headers, and footers.\\r\\n- Track extraction progress and page failures.\\r\\n- Respect authentication boundaries.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-lead-gen \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-lead-gen\\r\\ndescription: Generate structured lead lists from prospect databases and web directories with Firecrawl browser. Use for finding prospects by role, company type, industry, stage, location, technologies, or other criteria and exporting CRM-ready JSON or CSV.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Lead Gen\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this to extract legitimately accessible prospect lists.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer the prospect target, source, lead count, and output format from context. If the target is clear, proceed immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise questions only if blocked, such as the prospect definition, source/auth requirement, or target lead count.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl browser for databases requiring filters, search forms, pagination, or login. Use search/scrape for public sources.\\r\\n\\r\\nApply filters such as role, company size, industry, geography, funding stage, and technologies when available.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Extraction Fields\\r\\n\\r\\nCapture visible or legitimately accessible fields:\\r\\n\\r\\n- name\\r\\n- title\\r\\n- company\\r\\n- company URL\\r\\n- location\\r\\n- email, phone, and LinkedIn only when visible/allowed\\r\\n- industry, company size, funding stage\\r\\n- notes and profile URL\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# Lead List: [Target]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Summary\\r\\n[Source, filters, count, caveats]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Leads\\r\\n[Table or link to JSON/CSV]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Data Gaps\\r\\n[Masked, unavailable, or paywalled fields]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-lead-gen\\r\\ntarget: [description]\\r\\nsource: [auto/source/url]\\r\\nmax_leads: [number]\\r\\noutput: [json/csv/markdown]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Only extract publicly visible or legitimately accessible data.\\r\\n- Note masked, unavailable, or paywalled fields.\\r\\n- Deduplicate leads.\\r\\n- Do not bypass CAPTCHAs or access controls.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-lead-research \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-lead-research\\r\\ndescription: Produce pre-meeting lead intelligence briefs with Firecrawl. Use when the user needs company research, person research, recent news, talking points, pain points, or outreach preparation before a sales call, partnership meeting, investor conversation, or customer interview.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Lead Research\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this to create a concise, actionable pre-meeting brief.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer the company, person, meeting context, and desired brief depth from context. If the company is clear, proceed immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise questions only if blocked, such as the company/person to research or the meeting context.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl search and scrape to gather:\\r\\n\\r\\n- company website, about, product, pricing, careers, team, and customer pages\\r\\n- recent news, funding, launches, hiring, partnerships, and press\\r\\n- public person profiles, talks, posts, interviews, and role/background\\r\\n- relevant industry context and likely business challenges\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parallel Work\\r\\n\\r\\nIf appropriate, use sub-agents or equivalent parallel task runners:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Company Profile researcher\\r\\n- Recent News and Activity researcher\\r\\n- Person researcher\\r\\n- Industry/Pain Point researcher\\r\\n\\r\\nEach researcher should return source URLs and only evidence-backed claims.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# Lead Brief: [Company]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Company Overview\\r\\n[What they do, stage/size signals, products, customers]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Recent Activity\\r\\n[News, launches, funding, hiring, partnerships]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Key People\\r\\n[Relevant people and public background]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Talking Points\\r\\n[5-7 specific conversation starters]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Likely Pain Points\\r\\n[Evidence-backed hypotheses]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Outreach Angle\\r\\n[Suggested positioning or next step]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Sources\\r\\n[URLs used]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-lead-research\\r\\ncompany: [name/url]\\r\\nperson: [optional]\\r\\ncontext: [meeting context]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Keep it concise and useful before a meeting.\\r\\n- Do not fabricate personal details.\\r\\n- Clearly separate facts from inferred pain points.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-market-research \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-market-research\\r\\ndescription: Extract market, financial, earnings, industry, and company metrics with Firecrawl. Use when the user asks for market research, industry trends, public company data, financial comparisons, earnings research, or structured market reports.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Market Research\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this for sourced market and financial research.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer the market/company, data focus, timeframe, and output format from context. If the research target is clear, proceed immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise questions only if blocked, such as the market/company, required data focus, or timeframe/geography.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl search and scrape for market reports, news, investor relations, SEC filings, and company pages. Use browser where charts, tabs, period selectors, or financial portals require interaction.\\r\\n\\r\\nCommon sources include company investor relations pages, SEC filings, financial portals, earnings releases, industry reports, and news.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parallel Work\\r\\n\\r\\nIf appropriate, use sub-agents or equivalent parallel task runners:\\r\\n\\r\\n- company financials\\r\\n- market metrics\\r\\n- industry trends\\r\\n- recent news and analyst commentary\\r\\n- source validation\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# Market Research: [Market]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Market Overview\\r\\n[Industry description, size, growth, key players]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Company Profiles\\r\\n[Financial summary, market metrics, recent developments]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Comparison Tables\\r\\n[Revenue, margins, valuation multiples, growth]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Trends And Outlook\\r\\n[Industry trends, forecasts, risks]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Sources\\r\\n[URLs and data extracted]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-market-research\\r\\nquery: [market/company]\\r\\ncompanies: [list]\\r\\ndata_points: [all/financial/metrics/trends]\\r\\noutput: [json/markdown]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Cross-reference key numbers when possible.\\r\\n- Note conflicting data across sources.\\r\\n- Include period and unit for every metric.\\r\\n- Do not provide financial advice.\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]

## Tool: exec

text(await tools.exec_command({cmd:"python -X utf8 -c \"import json,pathlib;d=json.load(open('audits/skills-2026-10-09/global-review.json',encoding='utf-8'));names=['firecrawl-deep-research','firecrawl-download','firecrawl-interact','firecrawl-monitor','firecrawl-qa','firecrawl-workflows'];[(print('\\nFILE',n,'\\n',pathlib.Path(next(x['path'] for x in d['rows'] if x['name']==n)).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))) for n in names]\"",max_output_tokens:6500}));


## Tool: custom_tool_call_output

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 4.9 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"4cd78b","wall_time_seconds":0.526712,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":6059,"output":"\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-deep-research \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-deep-research\\r\\ndescription: |\\r\\n  Produce an intensive, cited analytical report: executive summary, multi-angle\\r\\n  findings, contrarian views, open questions, and full sources. Use only when the\\r\\n  user needs rigorous synthesis of a complex topic (scientific, technical, policy,\\r\\n  or market-analytical) that cannot be answered with a short search, and wants\\r\\n  a formal written report, not a recommendation list.\\r\\n\\r\\n  Do not use for product picks, top-N lists, quick lookups, or routine \\"find out\\r\\n  about X\\" tasks. If the request does not clearly need this kind of report, do\\r\\n  not use this skill.\\r\\n\\r\\n  Do not use for a literature review over published papers. This skill collects\\r\\n  evidence from the open web. A request for the literature on a biomedical,\\r\\n  clinical, life-science, or other scientific topic — papers, studies, trials,\\r\\n  preprints — belongs to firecrawl-research-papers, which queries Firecrawl\'s\\r\\n  paper index (PubMed, bioRxiv, medRxiv, arXiv) instead of searching websites.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Deep Research\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this only for report-scale research: a rigorous, cited synthesis the user\\r\\nexplicitly wants delivered as a formal written report. If the request is a\\r\\nproduct pick, a top-N list, a quick lookup, or anything answerable with a short\\r\\nsearch, stop; do not use this skill, let the request be handled the standard\\r\\nway.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis skill gathers its evidence from the open web. If the evidence base is the\\r\\npublished literature — a literature review, or a biomedical, clinical, or other\\r\\nscientific topic where the answer lives in papers — use\\r\\n[firecrawl-research-papers](../firecrawl-research-papers/SKILL.md) instead; it\\r\\nqueries Firecrawl\'s paper index rather than searching websites.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer the topic and output format from context. Before starting, unless already specified, always ask one short question to define the scope:\\r\\n\\r\\n> \\"How long do you want this research task to run?\\"\\r\\n\\r\\nMap the answer to a depth tier in the Collection Plan below:\\r\\n- A few minutes → Quick\\r\\n- ~10-15 minutes → Thorough\\r\\n- Longer / no limit → Exhaustive\\r\\n\\r\\nIf the topic itself is unclear, you may ask at most 1-2 additional concise\\r\\nquestions (topic, or a critical angle/source constraint). Otherwise proceed once\\r\\nthe runtime is set.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl search and scrape through the CLI or equivalent tool surface. Match\\r\\ndepth to the runtime the user chose during onboarding.\\r\\n\\r\\n- Quick (~a few minutes): search 3-5 queries and scrape 5-10 high-quality sources.\\r\\n- Thorough (~10-15 minutes): search 5-10 queries from different angles and scrape 15-25 sources.\\r\\n- Exhaustive (longer): search 10+ queries and scrape 25+ sources, including primary sources, research papers, expert views, and contrarian sources.\\r\\n\\r\\nAvoid re-scraping URLs already returned with full content from a search-with-scrape result.\\r\\n\\r\\n### When Published Papers Are The Evidence\\r\\n\\r\\nSearch and scrape reach web pages. They do not query Firecrawl\'s research paper\\r\\nindex, which holds paper abstracts with full text reachable per paper — largely\\r\\nbiomedical and life-science literature from PubMed, bioRxiv, and medRxiv, plus\\r\\narXiv preprints in CS, physics, and math.\\r\\n\\r\\nHand off to [firecrawl-research-papers](../firecrawl-research-papers/SKILL.md)\\r\\nwhen the report\'s evidence base is the published literature — a biomedical,\\r\\nclinical, drug, gene, disease, epidemiology, or public-health topic, or any\\r\\nrequest phrased as a literature review, systematic review, or survey of studies.\\r\\nThat skill uses `firecrawl_research_*` (MCP) / `firecrawl research` (CLI) to\\r\\nsearch abstracts, expand to related papers, and verify claims inside a paper\\r\\nbody — none of which plain search and scrape can do.\\r\\n\\r\\nIf the report needs both — the literature *and* market, policy, or news context —\\r\\nrun the paper work through that skill and keep the web collection above for the\\r\\nrest, then synthesize here.\\r\\n\\r\\nNote that passing `categories: [\\"research\\"]` to Firecrawl search does not query\\r\\nthe paper index either. It filters an ordinary web search to research-affiliated\\r\\nwebsites — the list includes PubMed, bioRxiv, medRxiv, arXiv, and publisher\\r\\nsites — and returns their web pages, not the paper records behind them.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parallel Work\\r\\n\\r\\nIf appropriate, use sub-agents or equivalent parallel task runners by research angle:\\r\\n\\r\\n- overview and definitions\\r\\n- technical or implementation details\\r\\n- market and industry context\\r\\n- contrarian views, risks, and limitations\\r\\n- primary sources and official docs\\r\\n\\r\\nEach researcher should return claims, source URLs, source quality notes, and uncertainty.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\nDefault structure:\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# Deep Research: [Topic]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Executive Summary\\r\\n[2-3 paragraphs]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Key Findings\\r\\n[Numbered findings with source links]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Detailed Analysis\\r\\n[Themes, evidence, and synthesis]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Contrarian Views And Risks\\r\\n[Counterarguments, limitations, failure modes]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Open Questions\\r\\n[What remains uncertain]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Sources\\r\\n[Every URL used with a one-line note]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-deep-research\\r\\ntopic: [topic]\\r\\ndepth: [quick/thorough/exhaustive]\\r\\noutput: [markdown/json/brief]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Cite sources for factual claims.\\r\\n- Prefer primary sources when available.\\r\\n- Flag uncertainty and conflicting evidence.\\r\\n- Synthesize instead of listing scrape summaries.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-download \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-download\\r\\ndescription: |\\r\\n  Save a site or section as local files (markdown, screenshots). Use for \\"download the site\\", offline docs, or a local copy for reference.\\r\\nallowed-tools:\\r\\n  - Bash(firecrawl *)\\r\\n  - Bash(npx firecrawl-cli *)\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# firecrawl download (invoked as `firecrawl x download`)\\r\\n\\r\\n> **Experimental.** `download` is available under the `firecrawl x` command group.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Prerequisite:** `download` requires authentication (no keyless free tier); without credentials the CLI prompts an interactive login.\\r\\n\\r\\nMaps the site origin first to discover pages, then scrapes each one into nested directories under `.firecrawl/`. Use `--include-paths` to scope a non-root URL to one section. Automated runs always pass `-y` — without it the command opens an interactive wizard that blocks on a prompt.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quick start\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# With screenshots\\r\\nfirecrawl x download https://docs.example.com --screenshot --limit 20 -y\\r\\n\\r\\n# Multiple formats (each saved as its own file per page)\\r\\nfirecrawl x download https://docs.example.com --format markdown,links --screenshot --limit 20 -y\\r\\n# Creates per page: index.md + links.txt + screenshot.png\\r\\n\\r\\n# Filter to specific sections\\r\\nfirecrawl x download https://docs.example.com --include-paths \\"/features,/sdks\\" -y\\r\\n\\r\\n# Skip translations\\r\\nfirecrawl x download https://docs.example.com --exclude-paths \\"/zh,/ja,/fr,/es,/pt-BR\\" -y\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nRun `firecrawl x download --help` for the full option list, including which scrape options download supports.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Done when:** the command exits successfully and the expected files exist under `.firecrawl/`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## See also\\r\\n\\r\\n- [firecrawl-map](../firecrawl-map/SKILL.md) — just discover URLs without downloading\\r\\n- [firecrawl-scrape](../firecrawl-scrape/SKILL.md) — scrape individual pages\\r\\n- [firecrawl-crawl](../firecrawl-crawl/SKILL.md) — bulk extract as JSON (not local files)\\r\\n- [firecrawl-build-scrape](https://github.com/firecrawl/skills/tree/main/skills/build/firecrawl-build-scrape) — building bulk extraction into an app instead of running it here\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-interact \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-interact\\r\\ndescription: |\\r\\n  Drive a live browser on a scraped page: click, fill forms, log in, paginate, infinite-scroll. Use when content requires interaction or a scrape failed or returned incomplete content.\\r\\nallowed-tools:\\r\\n  - Bash(firecrawl *)\\r\\n  - Bash(npx firecrawl-cli *)\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# firecrawl interact\\r\\n\\r\\nInteract with scraped pages in a live browser session. Scrape a page first, then use natural language prompts or code to click, fill forms, navigate, and extract data. For web searches, use `search` — interact is for acting on a specific page.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quick start\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# 1. Scrape a page (scrape ID is saved automatically)\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"<url>\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n# 2. Interact with the page using a positional prompt\\r\\nfirecrawl interact \\"Click the login button\\"\\r\\nfirecrawl interact \\"Fill in the email field with test@example.com\\"\\r\\nfirecrawl interact \\"Extract the pricing table\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n# A UUID first argument is auto-detected as the scrape ID\\r\\nfirecrawl interact \\"<scrape-id>\\" \\"Extract the pricing table\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n# 3. Or use code for precise control\\r\\nfirecrawl interact --code \\"agent-browser click @e5\\" --bash\\r\\nfirecrawl interact --code \\"agent-browser snapshot -i\\" --bash\\r\\n\\r\\n# 4. Stop the session when done\\r\\nfirecrawl interact stop\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nRun `firecrawl interact --help` for the full option list.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Done when:** the requested content or action result is captured and the session is stopped with `firecrawl interact stop`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Profiles\\r\\n\\r\\nUse `--profile` on the scrape to persist browser state (cookies, localStorage) across scrapes:\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# Session 1: Login and save state\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"https://app.example.com/login\\" --profile my-app\\r\\nfirecrawl interact --prompt \\"Fill in email with user@example.com and click login\\"\\r\\n\\r\\n# Session 2: Come back authenticated\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"https://app.example.com/dashboard\\" --profile my-app\\r\\nfirecrawl interact --prompt \\"Extract the dashboard data\\"\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nRead-only reconnect (no writes to profile state):\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\nfirecrawl scrape \\"https://app.example.com\\" --profile my-app --no-save-changes\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Tips\\r\\n\\r\\n- Always scrape first — `interact` requires a scrape ID from a previous `firecrawl scrape` call\\r\\n- The scrape ID is saved automatically, so you can omit `--scrape-id` for subsequent interact calls. Saved sessions may expire after about 10 minutes; re-scrape if the CLI warns that the session is stale\\r\\n- Use `firecrawl interact stop` to free resources when done\\r\\n- For parallel work, scrape multiple pages and interact with each using `--scrape-id`\\r\\n\\r\\n## See also\\r\\n\\r\\n- [firecrawl-scrape](../firecrawl-scrape/SKILL.md) — try scrape first, escalate to interact only when needed\\r\\n- [firecrawl-search](../firecrawl-search/SKILL.md) — use `search` for web searches\\r\\n- [firecrawl-agent](../firecrawl-agent/SKILL.md) — AI-powered extraction (less manual control)\\r\\n- [firecrawl-build-interact](https://github.com/firecrawl/skills/tree/main/skills/build/firecrawl-build-interact) — building interact into an app instead of running it here\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-monitor \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-monitor\\r\\ndescription: |\\r\\n  Alert by webhook/email on web changes — use for \\"monitor/watch/track/alert me when\\": recurring checks on known URLs (prefer over repeated one-off scrapes) or web-wide watches for new results (queries + goal).\\r\\nallowed-tools:\\r\\n  - Bash(firecrawl *)\\r\\n  - Bash(npx firecrawl-cli *)\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# firecrawl monitor\\r\\n\\r\\nDetect when content on a website changes and get notified by webhook or email. Firecrawl handles fetching, diffing, judging, and notifying server-side. Each page in a check is labeled `same`, `new`, `changed`, `removed`, or `error`.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Pick a target mode** by what you\'re watching:\\r\\n\\r\\n| Mode        | Flags                          | Watches                                                |\\r\\n| ----------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------ |\\r\\n| Single page | `--page <url>`                 | one URL, for changes                                   |\\r\\n| URL batch   | `--scrape-urls <url,url,...>`  | several URLs, for changes                              |\\r\\n| Whole site  | `--crawl-url <root-url>`       | every page a crawl discovers, for changes              |\\r\\n| Web search  | `--queries <q,...>` + `--goal` | the **whole web**, for _new_ results matching the goal |\\r\\n\\r\\nThe first three watch URLs you already have. **Web search** runs your queries each check and alerts on results it hasn\'t seen before (labeled `new` once, `same` on later checks); `--goal` is required with `--queries`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quick start\\r\\n\\r\\n```bash\\r\\n# Single page, natural-language schedule, email alert\\r\\nfirecrawl monitor create --name \\"Blog\\" --schedule \\"every 30 minutes\\" \\\\\\r\\n  --goal \\"Alert when a new blog post is published.\\" \\\\\\r\\n  --page https://example.com/blog \\\\\\r\\n  --email alerts@example.com\\r\\n\\r\\n# Web monitor — search the whole web for NEW results matching a goal\\r\\nfirecrawl monitor create --name \\"Competitor launches\\" --schedule \\"daily at 9:00\\" \\\\\\r\\n  --queries \\"competitor product launch,competitor funding round\\" \\\\\\r\\n  --goal \\"Alert when a competitor announces a new product or raises funding.\\" \\\\\\r\\n  --search-window 7d --max-results 20 \\\\\\r\\n  --email alerts@example.com\\r\\n\\r\\n# Webhook notifications\\r\\nfirecrawl monitor create --name \\"Docs webhook\\" --schedule \\"every 30 minutes\\" \\\\\\r\\n  --goal \\"Alert when docs content changes.\\" \\\\\\r\\n  --page https://example.com/docs \\\\\\r\\n  --webhook-url https://example.com/hook \\\\\\r\\n  --webhook-events monitor.page,monitor.check.completed\\r\\n\\r\\n# Manage and inspect\\r\\nfirecrawl monitor list --limit 20\\r\\nfirecrawl monitor get <monitorId>\\r\\nfirecrawl monitor run <monitorId>             # trigger a check now\\r\\nfirecrawl monitor checks <monitorId>          # list all checks\\r\\nfirecrawl monitor check <monitorId> <checkId> --page-status changed\\r\\nfirecrawl monitor update <monitorId> --state paused\\r\\nfirecrawl monitor delete <monitorId>\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\nSubcommands: `create | list | get | update | delete | run | checks | check`. Run `firecrawl monitor <subcommand> --help` for the full option list.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Done when:** `create` returns a monitor ID and a smoke-test `run` + `check` confirms the expected target, state, and notification configuration.\\r\\n\\r\\nRead [goals.md](goals.md) when writing or refining `--goal` (and `--queries` for web monitors). Read [json-tracking.md](json-tracking.md) when the user cares about specific structured fields (price, headline, stock flag) and wants per-field diffs.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Constraints & tips\\r\\n\\r\\n- Each check uses credits for its underlying scrape, crawl, or search, plus optional judging. See [Monitoring pricing](https://docs.firecrawl.dev/features/monitoring#pricing).\\r\\n- Minimum schedule interval is **5 minutes**. Monitoring is **not available for zero-data-retention teams**.\\r\\n- **Prefer one monitor over repeated one-off scrapes** whenever the user wants the same URL checked more than once.\\r\\n- **Silence temporarily with `update --state paused`**; reserve `delete` for monitors that are permanently done. (`--state` is an update flag; `--status` is the global CLI status flag.)\\r\\n- **Filter check pages with `--page-status changed`** (or `new`, `removed`, `error`) to skip the noise from `same` pages.\\r\\n- **`firecrawl monitor run <id>`** triggers a check immediately — useful for smoke-testing a monitor right after creating it.\\r\\n- **`--retention-days`** controls how long snapshots are kept for diffing. Lower it for high-frequency monitors to save storage.\\r\\n- **External email recipients must opt in.** First time they\'re added, Firecrawl sends a confirmation email and they only receive alerts after they confirm. Team-owned addresses are auto-confirmed. Once a recipient unsubscribes, they must be re-added by the owner for a fresh confirmation email.\\r\\n- **On HTTP 429 / rate-limit errors, back off once**: wait ~30s and retry once. If it persists, stop, report the rate limit as the blocking reason, and delete any monitors created for this task. Never retry in a loop.\\r\\n- **Monitor-triggered scrapes default `maxAge` to `0`** — every check performs a fresh scrape unless `scrapeOptions.maxAge` is set explicitly in a JSON payload.\\r\\n\\r\\n## See also\\r\\n\\r\\n- [firecrawl-scrape](../firecrawl-scrape/SKILL.md) — one-off scrape; escalate to `monitor` when checks become recurring\\r\\n- [firecrawl-crawl](../firecrawl-crawl/SKILL.md) — one-off crawl; pair with `--crawl-url` here for recurring crawl diffs\\r\\n- [firecrawl](../firecrawl/SKILL.md) — top-level workflow guide\\r\\n- [firecrawl-build-scrape](https://github.com/firecrawl/skills/tree/main/skills/build/firecrawl-build-scrape) — building recurring checks into an app instead of running it here\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-qa \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-qa\\r\\ndescription: QA test a live website with Firecrawl browser and scrape evidence. Use when the user wants exploratory QA, form testing, navigation/link checks, responsive checks, performance observations, bug reports, or a pre-launch quality review.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl QA\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this to test a live site and return a unified QA report.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Onboarding Interview\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer the URL, QA focus, and output format from context. If the target URL is clear, proceed immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise questions only if blocked, such as the URL, the focus area, or credentials/constraints for protected flows.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Firecrawl Collection Plan\\r\\n\\r\\nUse Firecrawl map to discover pages. Use Firecrawl browser for interactions, forms, navigation, and responsive/manual checks when available. Use scrape for page content and link extraction.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parallel Work\\r\\n\\r\\nIf appropriate, use sub-agents or equivalent parallel task runners:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Full: Navigation and Links, Forms and Interactions, Content and Visual, Error States.\\r\\n- Forms: Form Discovery, Happy Path, Edge Cases, Validation.\\r\\n- Navigation: Sitemap, Nav Testing, Link Checker, Routing.\\r\\n- Responsive: Desktop, Tablet, Mobile, Interaction.\\r\\n- Performance: Page Load, Asset Audit, Content Efficiency, Comparison.\\r\\n\\r\\nEach tester should return severity, URL, description, evidence, and reproduction steps.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Final Deliverable\\r\\n\\r\\n```markdown\\r\\n# QA Report: [Site]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Summary\\r\\n- Health score: [x/10]\\r\\n- Pages tested: [count]\\r\\n- Issues found: [critical/major/minor]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Critical Issues\\r\\n[C-1] URL | Description | Steps to reproduce | Expected vs actual\\r\\n\\r\\n## Major Issues\\r\\n[M-1] URL | Description | Steps to reproduce\\r\\n\\r\\n## Minor Issues\\r\\n[m-1] URL | Description\\r\\n\\r\\n## Positive Observations\\r\\n[What works well]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Pages Tested\\r\\n[URLs]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Agent/Test Summary\\r\\n[Who tested what]\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rerun Inputs\\r\\nworkflow: firecrawl-qa\\r\\nurl: [url]\\r\\nfocus: [full/forms/navigation/responsive/performance]\\r\\n```\\r\\n\\r\\n## Quality Bar\\r\\n\\r\\n- Include reproduction steps for functional issues.\\r\\n- Do not report speculative bugs without evidence.\\r\\n- Deduplicate findings across testers.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nFILE firecrawl-workflows \\r\\n ---\\r\\nname: firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ndescription: Run outcome-focused Firecrawl workflows that produce deliverables such as research reports, literature reviews over published papers, SEO audits, QA reports, lead lists, knowledge bases, website design systems, and other structured web-data artifacts. Use when the user wants Firecrawl to complete a business, marketing, product, or creative workflow rather than merely scrape a page or integrate API calls into code.\\r\\nlicense: ISC\\r\\nmetadata:\\r\\n  author: firecrawl\\r\\n  version: \\"0.1.0\\"\\r\\n  homepage: https://www.firecrawl.dev\\r\\n  source: https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-workflows\\r\\ninputs:\\r\\n  - name: FIRECRAWL_API_KEY\\r\\n    description: Firecrawl API key for hosted Firecrawl requests when the workflow runs through the CLI or API.\\r\\n    required: true\\r\\nreferences:\\r\\n  - references/workflow-authoring.md\\r\\n---\\r\\n\\r\\n# Firecrawl Workflows\\r\\n\\r\\nUse this when the user wants a finished deliverable powered by Firecrawl, not only raw web extraction and not product-code integration.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Choose The Workflow\\r\\n\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-website-design-clone](../firecrawl-website-design-clone/SKILL.md) to extract a website\'s colors, fonts, spacing, components, and layout patterns into an agent-ready `DESIGN.md`.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-research-papers](../firecrawl-research-papers/SKILL.md) for literature reviews and paper-backed synthesis, including biomedical, clinical, drug, gene, disease, epidemiology, and public-health topics. It queries Firecrawl\'s paper index — PubMed, bioRxiv, medRxiv, and arXiv abstracts with full text reachable per paper — rather than searching websites.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-deep-research](../firecrawl-deep-research/SKILL.md) for sourced multi-source research reports built from **web** evidence: market, policy, technical, or industry topics. Not for literature reviews — when the evidence base is published papers, use `firecrawl-research-papers` above.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-seo-audit](../firecrawl-seo-audit/SKILL.md) for site structure, on-page SEO, keyword, and SERP audits.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-lead-research](../firecrawl-lead-research/SKILL.md) for pre-meeting company/person intelligence briefs.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-qa](../firecrawl-qa/SKILL.md) for live-site QA testing and bug reports.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-competitive-intel](../firecrawl-competitive-intel/SKILL.md) for recurring pricing, feature, and changelog monitoring.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-company-directories](../firecrawl-company-directories/SKILL.md) for directory extraction into company lists.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-dashboard-reporting](../firecrawl-dashboard-reporting/SKILL.md) for dashboard metrics extraction.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-knowledge-base](../firecrawl-knowledge-base/SKILL.md) for LLM-ready docs, RAG chunks, training data, or docs mirrors.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-knowledge-ingest](../firecrawl-knowledge-ingest/SKILL.md) for auth-gated or JS-heavy docs portal ingestion.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-lead-gen](../firecrawl-lead-gen/SKILL.md) for prospect list generation.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-market-research](../firecrawl-market-research/SKILL.md) for market, financial, and industry research.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-demo-walkthrough](../firecrawl-demo-walkthrough/SKILL.md) for product flow walkthroughs and UX teardown reports.\\r\\n- Use [firecrawl-shop](../firecrawl-shop/SKILL.md) for product research and shopping recommendations.\\r\\n\\r\\nIf no existing workflow fits, use this generic process and produce a reusable pattern that could become a new skill.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Required Intake\\r\\n\\r\\nInfer the workflow, inputs, audience, and output format from the user\'s request and surrounding context. If enough is clear, start immediately.\\r\\n\\r\\nAsk at most 1-3 concise clarifying questions only when a missing input would block the work, such as:\\r\\n\\r\\n- the URL, company, topic, or source to analyze\\r\\n- the desired deliverable or output format\\r\\n- a constraint that would materially change the workflow\\r\\n\\r\\nUse the host agent\'s normal way to ask clarifying questions. Do not depend on a harness-specific function name.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Default Process\\r\\n\\r\\n1. Confirm the workflow and final artifact.\\r\\n2. Collect web evidence with Firecrawl through the CLI or equivalent Firecrawl tool surface.\\r\\n3. Save or cite source evidence so the final claims are traceable.\\r\\n4. Run independent research units in parallel when available.\\r\\n5. Synthesize findings into the requested deliverable.\\r\\n6. Include a short \\"rerun inputs\\" block when the workflow could be automated.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Parallel Work\\r\\n\\r\\nIf appropriate, use sub-agents or equivalent parallel task runners for independent units such as:\\r\\n\\r\\n- one competitor per researcher\\r\\n- one URL or page per researcher\\r\\n- one source category per researcher\\r\\n- one analysis dimension per reviewer\\r\\n\\r\\nKeep the handoff generic: provide the unit of work, source URLs or search terms, expected extracted fields, and output format.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Deliverable Standards\\r\\n\\r\\nEvery workflow should return:\\r\\n\\r\\n- a concise executive summary\\r\\n- the evidence base used\\r\\n- the analysis or artifact requested by the user\\r\\n- recommendations or next actions when useful\\r\\n- automation inputs for reruns\\r\\n\\r\\nFor authoring new workflow skills, see [workflow-authoring.md](references/workflow-authoring.md).\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]
